(1)	The release of a convicted person from punishment or serving of punishment, as well as mitigation of the imposed punishment, except for release from punishment or mitigation of punishment by way of amnesty and pardon, may be carried out only by a court in the cases and procedure specified by law.

(2)	The court may release a person who has not reached the age of majority from punishment in the cases provided for in this law, by imposing coercive measures of an educational nature.

(3)	The court may also release a person from punishment in the cases provided for in Section 58 of this law.

(4)	The court may release from serving of punishment a person who has committed a criminal violation or a less serious crime due to dependence on alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substances, if this person has consented to undergo treatment for dependence on alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substances. The punishment shall be executed if the person has not commenced treatment within the time period set by the court or has subsequently evaded treatment.

(5)	If a person who has been convicted of a criminal offence or for whom punishment has been imposed by a prosecutor's penal order, after the pronouncement of the judgment or after the prosecutor has issued the penal order, has fallen ill with a mental illness that has deprived them of the ability to understand their actions or to control them, the court shall release this person from serving of punishment. Coercive measures of a medical nature may be imposed on them in accordance with the conditions provided for in this law.

(6)	If a person who has been convicted of a criminal offence or for whom punishment has been imposed by a prosecutor's penal order suffers from another serious, incurable illness, the court may release this person from serving of punishment.
(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 13.12.2007. and 13.12.2012., which enter into force on 01.04.2013.)

60. Section. Reduction of Punishment in Exceptional Cases

If the convicted person has assisted in disclosing a crime committed by another person that is equally serious, more serious or more dangerous than the criminal offence committed by them, and this other person has been brought to criminal liability for this crime, the court may reduce the punishment imposed in the judgment, but replace the imposed life imprisonment with deprivation of liberty for twenty years.
(In the wording of the law of 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

61. Section. Conditional Early Release from Punishment

(1)	A person sentenced to deprivation of liberty, except short-term deprivation of liberty, may be conditionally released early from serving the principal punishment if there is reason to believe that after release they will be able to integrate into society without committing a criminal offence.

(2)	Taking into account the convicted person's personality and behaviour during the serving of the sentence, conditional early release from punishment may be determined if:

1)	the convicted person has achieved a certain resocialisation result;

2)	the convicted person has, according to their possibilities, voluntarily compensated for the harm caused by the criminal offence;

3)	the convicted person has opportunities after release to obtain means of subsistence by lawful means;

4)	the time period established in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments has elapsed since the imposition of punishment for violation of the regime of serving the sentence and there are no valid punishments for administrative offences committed during the execution of the deprivation of liberty sentence;

5)	the convicted person is addressing and is prepared to continue addressing their psychological problems that caused or could cause the commission of a criminal offence;

6)	the convicted person has consented to treatment for alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substance addiction if the criminal offence was committed due to alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substance addiction.

(21) When conditionally releasing early from punishment, electronic monitoring may be applied to the convicted person, observing the following conditions:

1)	the convicted person consents to electronic monitoring;

2)	the implementation of electronic monitoring is possible at the convicted person's place of residence;

3)	the application of electronic monitoring will promote the convicted person's integration into society.

(3)	Conditional early release from punishment may be initiated when the convicted person has actually served:

1)	not less than half of the imposed sentence for a less serious crime committed;

2)	not less than two-thirds of the imposed sentence if it was imposed for a serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been sentenced to deprivation of liberty for an intentional crime and the criminal record for this crime has not been removed or expunged;

3)	not less than three-quarters of the imposed sentence if it was imposed for a particularly serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been conditionally released early from punishment and has committed a new intentional crime during the period of the unserved part of the sentence;

4)	twenty-five years of the deprivation of liberty sentence if the convicted person is a person who has been sentenced to life imprisonment.

(31)	If, in accordance with the provisions of Section 2.1 of this Section, the application of electronic monitoring is possible, conditional early release from punishment with the imposition of electronic monitoring may be initiated when the convicted person has actually served:

1)	not less than one-third of the imposed sentence for a less serious crime;

2)	not less than half of the imposed sentence if it was imposed for a serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been sentenced to deprivation of liberty for an intentional crime and the criminal record for this crime has not been removed or expunged;

3)	not less than two-thirds of the imposed sentence if it was imposed for a particularly serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been conditionally released early from punishment and has committed a new intentional crime during the period of the unserved part of the sentence;

4)	not less than twenty-four years of the deprivation of liberty sentence if the convicted person is a person who has been sentenced to life imprisonment.

(4)	A person conditionally released early, during the period of the unserved part of the sentence, fulfils the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments and established by the sentence execution institution. If a person conditionally released early fails to fulfil the said obligations without justifiable reason, the court, based on an application from the sentence execution institution, may adopt a decision on the execution of the unserved part of the sentence.

(41)	If a person conditionally released early, to whom electronic monitoring has been applied, fails to fulfil the obligations related to electronic monitoring established in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments without justifiable reason, revokes their consent to electronic monitoring, or if in the circumstances in which they live the implementation of electronic monitoring is no longer possible, the court, based on an application from the sentence execution institution, adopts a decision on the execution of the unserved part of the sentence.

(42)	If a person conditionally released early, to whom electronic monitoring has been applied, has exemplarily fulfilled the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments and established by the sentence execution institution and the time period established in Part Three of Section 61 of this Law has occurred, according to which conditional early release from punishment without the imposition of electronic monitoring is possible, the court, based on an application from the sentence execution institution, may adopt a decision on the cancellation of electronic monitoring. If the court adopts a decision to cancel electronic monitoring, the convicted person is supervised in accordance with the supervision rules for convicted persons conditionally released early from serving the sentence provided for in the regulatory enactments regulating the execution of criminal punishments.

(43)	A person sentenced to life imprisonment who has been released from serving the sentence conditionally early, after release is under the supervision of the sentence execution institution for life and fulfils the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments and established by the sentence execution institution. If a person sentenced to life imprisonment who has been released from serving the sentence conditionally early fails to fulfil the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments and established by the sentence execution institution without justifiable reason, the court, based on an application from the sentence execution institution, may adopt a decision on the replacement of the early supervision period with deprivation of liberty for life (life imprisonment).

(44)	If a person sentenced to life imprisonment who has been released from serving the sentence conditionally early and to whom electronic monitoring has been applied, fails to fulfil the obligations related to electronic monitoring established in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments without justifiable reason, revokes their consent to electronic monitoring, or if in the circumstances in which they live the implementation of electronic monitoring is no longer possible, the court, based on an application from the sentence execution institution, adopts a decision on the replacement of the early supervision period with deprivation of liberty for life (life imprisonment).

(5)	If a person for whom conditional early release was determined commits a new criminal offence during the period of the unserved part of the sentence, the court imposes a sentence on them in accordance with the provisions set out in Sections 51 and 52 of this Law.

(6)	Conditional early release from punishment is not applied if it was imposed on an adult for a particularly serious crime committed against a person who has not reached the age of sixteen and is related to sexual violence.

(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 21.06.2007., 30.10.2008., 08.07.2011., 01.12.2011., 13.12.2012.,
15.05.2014., 16.10.2014., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

61. 1 Section. Electronic Monitoring

(1)	Electronic monitoring is intensive control measures determined by the court to restrict the convicted person's free movement. To implement electronic monitoring, an electronic device is attached to the convicted person's body, which allows control of their presence at a specified place and time.

(2)	The court determines electronic monitoring for a period from one month to twelve months.

(3)	The electronic monitoring period begins on the day when the electronic device is attached to the convicted person's body. Upon expiry of the electronic monitoring period determined by the court, the convicted person is supervised in accordance with the supervision rules for convicted persons conditionally released early from serving the sentence provided for in the regulatory enactments regulating the execution of criminal punishments, if the convicted person's unserved sentence period has not yet ended.
(As worded by the law of 16.10.2014., which enters into force on 01.02.2015. The Section enters into force on 01.07.2015. See transitional provision Point 19)

62. Section. Limitation Period for Execution of a Conviction Judgment

(1) A conviction judgment and a prosecutor's order on punishment shall not be executable if, from the day it entered into legal force, its execution has not been commenced within the following time limits:

1) two years — if short-term deprivation of liberty, community service or a fine has been imposed;

11) two years after serving the deprivation of liberty sentence — if the sentence — community service — is executable independently in the cases provided for in Section 52, Paragraph 2.1 of this Law;

2) (deleted by the Law of 06.06.2019);

21) three years — if probation supervision has been imposed;

3) five years — if deprivation of liberty for a period not exceeding five years has been imposed;

4) ten years — if deprivation of liberty for a period not exceeding ten years has been imposed;

5) fifteen years — if a more severe sentence than deprivation of liberty for ten years has been imposed.

(2) The limitation period shall be interrupted if the convicted person evades serving the sentence or, before the expiry of the limitation period, commits a new criminal offence for which the court has imposed deprivation of liberty for a period not less than one year. If a new criminal offence has been committed, the limitation period shall be calculated from the moment of its commission, but if the convicted person has evaded serving the sentence — from the moment when he or she arrives to serve the sentence or from the moment when the convicted person who has been hiding is detained. However, the conviction judgment shall not be executable if fifteen years have passed since its entry into force and the limitation period has not been interrupted by a new criminal offence. The question of whether the convicted person has evaded serving the sentence shall be decided by the court.

(3) The question of the limitation period for a person sentenced to life imprisonment shall be decided by the court.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 01.12.2011., 13.12.2012., 14.03.2013., 25.09.2014.,
29.10.2015., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

63. Section. Expungement and Removal of Criminal Record

(1) A criminal record is the legal consequence of conviction or imposition of a sentence on a person who has committed a criminal offence, which is in force during the period of serving the sentence determined by the court or in the prosecutor's order on punishment, as well as thereafter until the expungement or removal of the criminal record in the manner prescribed by law.

(2) A person shall be considered convicted from the moment a conviction judgment enters into force or from the day a prosecutor's order on punishment enters into force.

(3) The following shall be recognised as not having a criminal record:

1) persons who have been released from punishment by a court judgment or acquitted;

2) persons for whom the limitation period for execution of a conviction judgment provided for by law has occurred;

3) conditionally convicted persons — one year after the end of the probation period, but in the case of application of an additional sentence — one year after the end of the period of serving the additional sentence. If the imposed sentence is executed for a conditionally convicted person, the term for expungement of the criminal record shall be calculated taking into account the sentence actually served;

4) after one year — persons who have served short-term deprivation of liberty, community service or a fine;

5) after two years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty not exceeding three years, or probation supervision;

6) after five years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty exceeding three years but not exceeding five years;

7) after eight years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty exceeding five years but not exceeding ten years;

8) after ten years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty exceeding ten years.

(4) The term for expungement of a criminal record shall be calculated from the day the person has fully served the principal sentence and the additional sentence. The execution of the additional sentence — confiscation of property — shall not affect the calculation of the term for expungement of the criminal record.

(5) If a person has been released from punishment before the term in the manner prescribed by law, the term for expungement of the criminal record shall be calculated from the day he or she was released from serving the sentence, taking into account the period of actual serving of the sentence.

(6) (Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(7) If a person convicted with deprivation of liberty has, after serving the sentence, demonstrated his or her reform through exemplary conduct and honest attitude towards work, the court may remove the criminal record before the expiry of the terms specified in this Section.

(8) A criminal record may be removed by way of pardon or amnesty.

(9) Expungement and removal of a criminal record shall annul all criminal law consequences of the committed criminal offence, except those provided for by law if a new criminal offence has been committed before the expungement or removal of the criminal record.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 17.10.2002., 12.02.2004., 28.09.2005., 08.11.2007., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012.,
15.05.2014., 25.09.2014., 10.03.2016., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

VII Chapter
Specifics of Criminal Liability of a Minor
(Chapter title amended by the Law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

64. Section. Liability of a Minor

(1) The provisions of this Chapter shall apply to a person who has not reached the age of eighteen before committing a criminal offence.

(2) Other provisions of the General Part of this Law shall apply to a minor unless otherwise provided for in this Chapter.

(3) Taking into account the objective of punishment specified in the second paragraph of Section 35 of this Law, the resocialisation of the minor shall be primary in relation to him or her.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

65. Section. Types of Punishments for a Minor

(1) One of the following principal sentences may be imposed on a minor:

1) deprivation of liberty;

2) probation supervision;

3) community service;

4) fine.

(2) The following additional sentences may be imposed on a minor without a principal sentence:

1) probation supervision;

2) restriction of rights;

3) expulsion from the Republic of Latvia.

(3) A prosecutor, when drawing up an order on punishment, may impose probation supervision, community service or a fine, as well as an additional sentence — restriction of rights.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

66. Section. Application of Educational Coercive Measures to Minors

(1)	Taking into account the special circumstances of the commission of the criminal offence and the information obtained about the guilty person's personality that mitigates his or her liability, the court may release the minor from the imposed sentence by applying the educational coercive measures provided for by law.

(2)	The sentence shall be executed if the minor who has been released from it fails to fulfil the obligations imposed by the court during the period of the imposed sentence.
(With amendments made by the Law of 31.10.2002, which enters into force on 01.01.2003.)

66.1 Section. Application of Deprivation of Liberty

(1)	Deprivation of liberty is the placement of a minor in an educational institution and includes a set of social behaviour correction and social rehabilitation measures and rules of conduct provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences appropriate to the minor's age, psychological characteristics and level of development.

(2)	The term of deprivation of liberty may not exceed ten years — for particularly serious crimes; five years — for serious crimes involving violence or threat of violence or causing serious consequences; two years — for other serious crimes.

(3)	Deprivation of liberty shall not be applied for criminal violations and less serious crimes.

(4)	If a person has committed a criminal offence before reaching the age of eighteen for which the minimum limit of the applicable deprivation of liberty sentence is provided for in the sanction of the relevant article of the Special Part of this law, the court may impose a sentence that is lower than this minimum limit, even in cases where it has found that the criminal offence was committed in circumstances aggravating liability.

(5)	Deprivation of liberty for a minor shall always be applied together with an additional sentence — probation supervision, also in cases where this additional sentence is not provided for in the sanction of the relevant article of the Special Part of this law.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

66.2 Section. Application of Probation Supervision

(1)	In accordance with the harmfulness of the criminal offence, the court may impose probation supervision as a principal sentence on a minor for a period from three years to six years also for the commission of a serious crime for which this law provides for deprivation of liberty for a period longer than five years, and for the commission of a particularly serious crime.

(2)	The court, on the basis of a submission by the sentence execution institution, may adopt a decision that a minor for whom probation supervision has been imposed as a sentence by a court judgment or prosecutor's order on punishment shall be placed in a social correction educational institution for a period of up to three years, but not less than one year, taking into account the unfavourable social environment in which the minor is located, possible threat to the minor's health or life, or other reasons that hinder the execution of probation supervision.

(3)	If a minor for whom probation supervision has been imposed as a sentence by a court judgment or prosecutor's order on punishment or for whom community service has been replaced with probation supervision in the manner provided for in this law, without justifiable reason fails to fulfil the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences or imposed by the sentence execution institution, the court, on the basis of a submission by the sentence execution institution, may replace the unserved term of the sentence with deprivation of liberty, counting two days of probation supervision as one day of deprivation of liberty.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

66.3 Section. Replacement of Community Service

If a minor who has been sentenced to community service or a minor for whom community service has been imposed by a prosecutor's order on punishment, without justifiable reason fails to perform the community service, the court, on the basis of a submission by the sentence execution institution, shall replace the unserved sentence with probation supervision for one year.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

66.4 Section. Application of Fine

(1)	A fine shall be applicable only to those minors who have their own income. A fine for minors shall be applicable from one to fifty minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia.

(2)	A fine not paid within the specified time shall be replaced for a minor with community service, counting one minimum monthly wage as forty hours of community service, but not more than two hundred and eighty hours.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.12.2020 with amendments made by the Law of 07.04.2022, which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

66.	5 Section. Other Issues Related to the Imposition of Sentence

(1)	For a person who has committed a criminal offence before reaching the age of eighteen, the sentence for several criminal offences or under several judgments shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions of Sections 50 and 51 of this law, observing that the total term of deprivation of liberty for several criminal offences may not exceed twelve years and six months, and under several judgments — fifteen years.

(2)	Conditional early release from sentence may be initiated if the person has served not less than half of the imposed term of deprivation of liberty.

(3)	Conditional early release from sentence with the imposition of electronic monitoring may be initiated if the person has served not less than one third of the imposed term of deprivation of liberty.

(4)	A person who has committed a criminal violation or a less serious crime before reaching the age of eighteen shall be considered not convicted after serving the sentence.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

67. Section. Conditional Conviction of a Minor

(Deleted by the Law of 21.05.2009, which enters into force on 01.07.2009.)

VIII	Chapter
Coercive Measures of a Medical Nature

68. Section. Coercive Measures of a Medical Nature

(1)	For persons who have committed the offences provided for in this law but suffer from mental disorders and have been found to be non-imputable or of limited imputability, the following coercive measures of a medical nature may be imposed:

1)	outpatient treatment in a medical institution;

2)	treatment in a general-type psychiatric hospital (department);

3)	treatment in a specialised psychiatric hospital (department) with security.

(2)	If the person referred to in paragraph one of this Section, due to the nature of the offence committed and his or her mental state, is not dangerous to society, the court may place him or her in the care of relatives or other persons who provide care for the sick.

(3)	For the persons referred to in paragraph one of this Section who have been found to be of limited imputability, treatment may also be imposed in places of deprivation of liberty appropriate for them.
(With amendments made by the Law of 15.05.2014, which enters into force on 14.06.2014.)

69. pants

Imposition of Coercive Measures of a Medical Nature on Persons in a State of Non-Imputability

(1)	The court may impose the coercive measures of a medical nature provided for in this Law on persons who have committed the offences provided for in this Law while in a state of non-imputability, or who have fallen ill with a mental illness after committing the offence or after the pronouncement of the judgment that has deprived them of the ability to understand their actions or to control them, if these persons are dangerous to society due to the nature of the offence committed and their mental state.

(2)	Compulsory treatment and the type of medical institution shall be determined by the court depending on the mental illness with which the person concerned has fallen ill and the nature of the offence committed by that person. When determining treatment in a psychiatric hospital (ward), the type thereof shall be chosen by the medical institution.

(3)	A person who has fallen ill with a mental illness after committing a criminal offence or after the pronouncement of the judgment, which has deprived that person of the ability to understand their actions or to control them, may be sentenced by the court after recovery if the limitation period has not expired or there is no other ground for releasing that person from criminal liability and punishment.

(4)	The court shall terminate the application of a coercive measure of a medical nature or change it to a less restrictive measure if the person concerned has recovered or their state of health has improved, or if it is established that the state of health of that person has otherwise changed to such an extent that they are no longer dangerous to society.

(41) The court may change a coercive measure of a medical nature to a more restrictive measure if the person fails to comply with the coercive measure of a medical nature imposed on them.

(5)	If such a person is sentenced to a punishment after recovery, the time during which coercive measures of a medical nature have been applied shall be included in the term of the punishment.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 15.05.2014 and 25.09.2014, which enter into force on 29.10.2014.)

69.	1 pants. Imposition of a Coercive Measure of a Medical Nature Following Several Decisions

If a person for whom a coercive measure of a medical nature has been imposed commits a new criminal offence after its imposition, for which a coercive measure of a medical nature is imposed on them, the court shall add to the coercive measure of a medical nature imposed in the new decision the previously imposed coercive measure of a medical nature, incorporating the less restrictive coercive measure of a medical nature into the more restrictive measure.
(In the wording of the Law of 15.05.2014, which enters into force on 14.06.2014.)

70. pants

Imposition of Coercive Measures of a Medical Nature on Persons in a State of Diminished Imputability

Coercive measures of a medical nature may also be imposed on persons who have committed criminal offences while in a state of diminished imputability. If such a person is sentenced to deprivation of liberty, treatment shall take place in the places of deprivation of liberty applied to them. If such a person is sentenced without deprivation of liberty, the court shall impose on them the

obligation to receive treatment at a psychiatric medical institution at their place of residence.

VIII1 nodaļa
Coercive Measures Applicable to Legal Persons

(Chapter 05

Version of 2005 law, which comes into force on 01.10.2005.)

Article 70.1 Grounds for Application of Coercive Measure to a Legal Person

For a criminal offence provided for in the Special Part of this Law, the court or, in cases provided for by law, the prosecutor may apply a coercive measure to a private law legal person, including a state or municipal capital company, as well as to a partnership, if the offence has been committed in the interests of the legal person, for the benefit of this person or as a result of its inadequate supervision or control by a natural person acting individually or as a member of a collegial institution of the respective legal person:

1) on the basis of the right to represent the legal person or act on its behalf;

2) on the basis of the right to make decisions in the name of the legal person;

3) exercising control within the legal person.
(Version of 14.03.2013. law, which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.2 Types of Coercive Measures Applicable to a Legal Person

(1) The following coercive measures may be imposed on a legal person:

1) liquidation;

2) restriction of rights;

3) confiscation of property;

4) monetary exaction.

(2) One or more coercive measures provided for in paragraph one of this Article may be applied to a legal person. When applying liquidation, other coercive measures shall not be imposed.

(3) The procedure for execution of coercive measures shall be determined in accordance with law.

(4) For a criminal violation, less serious crime or serious crime provided for in the Special Part of this Law, for which imprisonment for up to five years is provided, the prosecutor, by drawing up a prescription on a coercive measure, may impose on a legal person a monetary exaction or restriction of rights as a coercive measure.
(Version of 14.03.2013. law with amendments made by 10.03.2016. law, which comes into force on 07.04.2016.)

Article 70.3 Liquidation

(1) Liquidation is the compulsory termination of the activities of a legal person.

(2) A legal person shall be liquidated only in cases where the legal person has been specially created for the commission of a criminal offence or where a serious or particularly serious crime has been committed.

(3) Upon liquidation of a legal person, all property owned by it shall be expropriated without compensation into state ownership.
(With amendments made by 14.03.2013. law, which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.4 Restriction of Rights

(1) Restriction of rights is the withdrawal of certain rights or permits or the imposition of a prohibition that prevents a legal person from exercising certain rights, receiving state support or assistance, participating in state or municipal procurement procedures, carrying out certain types of activities for a period from one year to ten years.

(2) (Deleted by 10.03.2016. law)
(Version of 14.03.2013. law with amendments made by 10.03.2016. law, which comes into force on 07.04.2016.)

Article 70.5 Confiscation of Property

(1) Confiscation of property is the compulsory expropriation without compensation into state ownership of property owned by a legal person.

(2) The court, when imposing confiscation of property, shall specifically indicate which property is to be confiscated.

(3) (Deleted by 14.03.2013. law)

(4) Property owned by a legal person that has been transferred to another person may also be confiscated.
(With amendments made by 13.12.2012. and 14.03.2013. law, which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.6 Monetary Exaction

(1) Monetary exaction is a sum of money that the court or prosecutor orders to be paid to the benefit of the state within 30 days in the amount specified in this Article.

(11) Monetary exaction, according to the harmfulness of the criminal offence and the financial situation of the legal person, shall be imposed:

1) for a criminal violation — in the amount of five to ten thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia;

2) for a less serious crime — in the amount of ten to fifty thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia;

3) for a serious crime — in the amount of twenty to seventy-five thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia;

4) for a particularly serious crime — in the amount of thirty to one hundred thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia.

(12) At the time of making the decision, the amount of monetary exaction shall be indicated in the decision in the monetary units of the Republic of Latvia.

(2) Monetary exaction imposed on a legal person shall be paid from the funds of this person.

(3) Payment of monetary exaction may be divided into instalments or postponed by the court or prosecutor respectively for a period not exceeding one year from the day when the decision or prescription on a coercive measure has entered into legal force.

(4) If the monetary exaction is not paid, the coercive measure shall be executed by compulsion.
(Version of 14.03.2013. law with amendments made by 29.10.2015. and 07.04.2022. law, which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

Article 70.7 Compensation for Harm
(Deleted by 14.03.2013. law, which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.8 Conditions for Application of Coercive Measures to a Legal Person

(1) When determining the type of coercive measure, the nature of the criminal offence, the harm caused and whether a coercive measure has previously been applied to the legal person shall be taken into account.

(2) When determining the extent of a coercive measure, the following conditions shall be observed:

1) the actual conduct of the legal person;

2) the nature and consequences of the activities of the legal person;

3) measures taken by the legal person to prevent the commission of a criminal offence;

4) the size, type of occupation and financial situation of the legal person;

5) measures taken by the legal person to compensate for the loss caused or eliminate the harm caused;

6) whether the legal person has reconciled with the victim.

(3) If several coercive measures have been applied to a legal person in accordance with paragraph two of Article 70.2 of this Law, each of them shall be executed independently.

(4) In a criminal case concerning several independent criminal offences, the court, when making a judgment, or the prosecutor, when drawing up a prescription on a coercive measure, shall impose a coercive measure on the legal person separately for each criminal offence. In such a case, the final set of coercive measures to be applied shall be determined according to the aggregate of criminal offences in the following order:

1) by absorbing the lighter monetary exaction into the more serious one or by adding them fully or partially;

2) by adding restrictions of rights fully;

3) by adding confiscations of property fully.

(5) If a legal person, after the judgment or prosecutor's prescription on a coercive measure has entered into force but before complete execution of the coercive measure, is involved in a new criminal proceeding concerning the application of a coercive measure, the court in the new judgment shall add to the coercive measure imposed the coercive measure that has not been executed under the previous judgment or prosecutor's prescription on a coercive measure, in the following order:

1) by adding monetary exactions fully or partially;

2) by adding restrictions of rights fully;

3) by adding confiscations of property fully.

(6) If liquidation has been applied, then in the cases referred to in paragraphs four and five of this Article, other coercive measures shall be absorbed into the liquidation.

(7) In the cases referred to in paragraphs four and five of this Article, the total amount or time of the coercive measure may exceed the maximum amount or time of the coercive measure provided for the most serious of the criminal offences committed, but not by more than half of the maximum amount or time of the coercive measure provided for the most serious of the criminal offences committed.
(Version of 14.03.2013. law with amendments made by 29.10.2015., 11.06.2020. and 07.04.2022. law, which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

Article 70.9 Calculation of Limitation Period

(1) In proceedings concerning the application of a coercive measure, the limitation period shall be calculated from the day when the criminal offence was committed until the moment when the legal person is notified of the initiation of proceedings concerning the application of a coercive measure, observing the time limits specified in points 2 to 6 of paragraph one of Article 56 and in Article 57 of this Law.

(2) A judgment or prosecutor's prescription on the application of a coercive measure shall not be enforceable if five years have passed from the day it entered into legal force.
(Version of 15.05.2014. law, which comes into force on 14.06.2014.)

Chapter VIII2 Special Confiscation of Property

(Chapter 22

06.2017. law version, which enters into force 01.08.2017. See transitional provisions point 21)

70.10 Article. Concept of special confiscation of property

Special confiscation of property is the compulsory uncompensated alienation to state ownership of criminally acquired property or an object of commission of a criminal offence, or property related to a criminal offence. Special confiscation of property is not a criminal punishment.
(22.06.2017. law version, which enters into force 01.08.2017.)

70.11 Article. Confiscation of criminally acquired property

(1)	Criminally acquired property is any economic benefit that has come into a person's ownership or possession directly or indirectly as a result of the commission of a criminal offence.

(11) Indirectly criminally acquired property is any economic benefit that has come into a person's ownership or possession as a result of further use of directly criminally acquired property, including as a result of reinvestment or transformation, or funds that a person has obtained from the realisation of such property, as well as fruits and profits obtained.

(2)	If the value of property is not commensurate with a person's lawful income and the person does not prove that the property was acquired lawfully, property that belongs to a person who:

1)	has committed a crime that by its nature is directed at obtaining material or other benefit, regardless of whether material or other benefit was obtained as a result of the crime;

2)	is a member of an organised group;

3)	has committed a crime related to terrorism,

may also be recognised as criminally acquired property.

(3)	Property that is in the possession of another such person who maintains permanent family, economic or other property relations with the person referred to in paragraph two of this Article may also be recognised as criminally acquired property, if the value of the property in this person's possession is not commensurate with their lawful income and if the person does not prove that the property was acquired lawfully.

(4)	Criminally acquired property shall be confiscated if it is not to be returned to the owner or lawful possessor.
(22.06.2017. law version with amendments made by 11.06.2020. and 07.04.2022. law, which enters into force 04.05.2022.)

70.12 Article. Confiscation of an object of commission of a criminal offence

(1)	An object of commission of a criminal offence is tools and means that were intended or were used for the commission of a criminal offence.

(2)	Objects of commission of a criminal offence shall be confiscated.
(22.06.2017. law version, which enters into force 01.08.2017.)

70.13 Article. Confiscation of property related to a criminal offence

(1)	Property related to a criminal offence is items prohibited from circulation or items whose origin or ownership in the particular criminal case has not been established, or such property belonging to a person who has committed a criminal offence that

due to the criminal offence committed may not be left in the person's ownership.

(2)	Property related to a criminal offence shall be confiscated.

(3)	Animals may be confiscated if due to the criminal offence committed they may not be left in the ownership of a person who has committed a criminal offence.

(4)	A vehicle belonging to a person who has committed a criminal offence may be subject to confiscation if a criminal offence against traffic safety has been committed.

(5)	A vehicle belonging to a person who has committed a criminal offence, with which they have committed the criminal offence provided for in Article 262. or 262.1 of this law, shall be subject to confiscation.
(22.06.2017. law version with amendments made by 27.10.2022. law, which enters into force 25.11.2022.)

70.14 Article. Replacement of property to be confiscated

(1)	If an object of commission of a criminal offence belongs to another person, the value of the object of commission of a criminal offence may be recovered.

(11) If the criminal offence provided for in Article 262. or 262.1 of this law has been committed with a vehicle that belongs to another person, the full or partial value of this vehicle shall be recovered from the person who committed the criminal offence.

(2)	If criminally acquired property has been alienated, destroyed, concealed or disguised and it is not possible to confiscate it, the value of the property to be confiscated may be recovered.

(3)	If confiscation of criminally acquired property has been applied to a person, the property to be confiscated may be replaced with financial means in the value of this property. Property that has historical, artistic or scientific value may not be replaced.

(4)	If it is not possible to confiscate criminally acquired property because it has been alienated, destroyed, concealed or disguised, and a person who has committed a criminal offence has no other property on which to direct recovery, the following may be confiscated:

1)	property that a person has alienated to another person after the commencement of a criminal offence without payment or for a value that is substantially lower or substantially higher than the market value;

2)	joint property of a person who has committed a criminal offence and their spouse, unless separation of spouses' property was established at least one year before the commencement of a criminal offence;

3)	property that belongs to another person with whom a person who has committed a criminal offence has a joint (undivided) household, if this property was acquired after the commencement of a criminal offence.
(22.06.2017. law version with amendments made by 27.10.2022. law, which enters into force 25.11.2022.)

SPECIAL PART

IX	Chapter
Crimes against humanity, peace, war crimes, genocide

71.Article. Genocide

For genocide, that is, for intentional acts with the purpose of wholly or partially destroying any national, ethnic, racial or particular religious group of people as such, by killing members of such group, causing them bodily injuries dangerous to life or health or bringing them to mental illness, intentionally creating for them such living conditions that wholly or partially physically destroy these people, using means whose purpose is to prevent the birth of children in such group, or forcibly transferring children from one group of people to another, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.
(With amendments made by 21.05.2009. law, which enters into force 01.07.2009.)

71.1 Article. Incitement to genocide

For public incitement to genocide —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(28.04.2005. law version, which enters into force 01.06.2005.)

71.	2 Article. Crimes against humanity

For a crime against humanity, that is, for an act committed as part of a widespread or systematic attack against civilians and manifested as murder, extermination, enslavement, deportation or forced displacement, unlawful deprivation or restriction of liberty, torture, rape, involvement of a person in sexual slavery, forcing to engage in prostitution, forced impregnation or sterilisation or other sexual violence of similar gravity, apartheid, persecution of any group of people or association based on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious or gender affiliation or other grounds recognised as impermissible in international law, in connection with any act specified in this Article, or genocide, or war crime, or other act provided for in international law binding on the Republic of Latvia that causes serious physical or mental suffering, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.
(21.05.2009. law version, which enters into force 01.07.2009.)

72. Article. Crimes against peace

For crimes committed against peace, that is, for planning, preparing, initiating, participating in aggression, for waging an aggressive war in violation of international treaties binding on the Republic of Latvia, for participating in a conspiracy with the purpose of committing the crimes referred to in this Article —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.
(With amendments made by 16.10.2014. law, which enters into force 01.02.2015.)

73. pants

Manufacturing, Storage, Transportation, Use and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction

For the manufacturing, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of nuclear, chemical, biological, bacteriological, toxic or other weapons of mass destruction —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.
(With amendments made by the law of 19.11.2009, which comes into force on 23.12.2009.)

73.1 pants. Financing of Manufacturing, Storage, Transportation, Use and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction

(1)	For the financing of manufacturing, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, that is, the direct or indirect collection or transfer of financial resources or other property obtained in any manner with the aim of using them or knowing that they will be used in whole or in part to finance the manufacturing, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the financing of manufacturing, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, if it has been committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of fifteen to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(In the wording of the law of 10.03.2016 with amendments made by the law of 26.04.2018, which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

74.pants. War Crimes

For war crimes, that is, for violation of the rules of warfare or international humanitarian law prohibited in international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, including the murder, torture or inhuman treatment of a person protected by humanitarian law, hostage-taking, unlawful deportation, displacement, restriction of liberty, unjustified destruction of cities or other objects or other prohibited acts —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.
(With amendments made by the law of 21.05.2009, which comes into force on 01.07.2009.)

74.	1 pants. Justification of Genocide, Crime Against Humanity, Crime Against Peace and War Crime

(1)	For public glorification of genocide, crime against humanity, crime against peace or war crime or public glorification, denial, justification or gross trivialisation of committed genocide, crime against humanity, crime against peace or war crime, including genocide, crime against humanity, crime against peace or war crime committed by the USSR or Nazi Germany against the Republic of Latvia and its inhabitants, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons or by a state official, or by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation or if they have been committed using an automated data processing system, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 17.12.2020. and 14.12.2023., which comes into force on 30.12.2023.)

75. pants. Violence Against Inhabitants in a Zone of Hostilities

For unlawful violence against inhabitants in a zone of hostilities, as well as for unlawful violent seizure or destruction of their property —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the law of 14.12.2023., which comes into force on 30.12.2023.)

76. pants. Marauding

For appropriation of property of the fallen or wounded on the battlefield (marauding) —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the law of 14.12.2023., which comes into force on 30.12.2023.)

77. pants. Incitement to Aggressive War

For public incitement to aggressive war or the provocation of a military conflict — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

77.1 pants. Unlawful Participation in an Armed Conflict

For unlawful participation in an armed conflict, that is, for active participation in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, which is directed against the territorial integrity or political independence of a state or is otherwise contrary to international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, without complying with regulatory enactments or international agreements binding on the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 12.02.2015., which comes into force on 19.02.2015.)

77.2 pants. Financing of an Armed Conflict

For the direct or indirect collection or transfer of financial resources or other property obtained in any manner to a party involved in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, whose activities are directed against the territorial integrity or political independence of a state or are otherwise contrary to international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, as well as for the recruitment, training or dispatch of a person so that they unlawfully participate in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 12.02.2015., which comes into force on 19.02.2015.)

77.3 pants. Recruitment, Training and Dispatch for an Armed Conflict

For the recruitment, training or dispatch of a person so that they unlawfully participate in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 12.02.2015., which comes into force on 19.02.2015.)

77.	4 pants. Assistance to a Foreign State in Activities Directed Against Another State

For activities with the aim of assisting a person, foreign state or foreign organisation to undermine or threaten the territorial integrity or political independence of another democratic state, or otherwise act against it contrary to international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, without complying with regulatory enactments or international agreements binding on the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 14.12.2023., which comes into force on 30.12.2023.)

78. pants. Incitement of National, Ethnic and Racial Hatred

(1)	For activities directed towards incitement of national, ethnic, racial or religious hatred or discord, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons or by a state official, or by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation or if they have been committed using an automated data processing system, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are connected with violence or threats or if they have been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

79. Section. Destruction of Cultural and National Heritage

For the intentional destruction of values that are part of cultural and national heritage, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to twelve years.
(With amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012, which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

Chapter IX1
Crimes Related to Terrorism

(Chapter 26

04.2018. law version, which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

Section 79.1 Terrorism

(1)	For explosions, arson, use of nuclear-chemical, chemical, biological, bacteriological, toxic or other weapons of mass destruction, mass poisoning, spreading of epidemics or epizootics, abduction of a person, hostage-taking, seizure of air, land or water transport, or other actions, if they are carried out with the intention to intimidate the population or to compel a state, its institutions or international organisations to perform any action or refrain from it, or to harm the interests of the state, its population or an international organisation (terrorism), —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For the destruction or damage of physical objects, automated data processing systems, electronic networks, as well as other objects located in the state territory or on the continental shelf, the purpose of which is to ensure state security, if such actions are carried out with the intention provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from ten to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(26.04.2018. law version, which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

Section 79.2 Financing of Terrorism

(1)	For financing of terrorism —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2)	For financing of terrorism, if it has been committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from ten to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(26.04.2018. law version, which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

Section 79.3 Terrorist Group

(1)	For organising a terrorist group, that is, a group of persons by prior agreement, for the purpose of committing one or more crimes related to terrorism, as well as for involvement in such a group —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to seventeen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For leading a terrorist group or participating in the crimes referred to in the first paragraph of this Section committed by such a group —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term from twelve to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(26.04.2018. law version, which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

Section 79.4 Recruitment, Training and Self-Training for Terrorism

(1)	For obtaining or receiving instructions, knowledge or practical skills for the purpose of carrying out or facilitating terrorism (self-training for terrorism) —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For recruitment of a person for terrorism or involvement in a terrorist group or training for terrorism —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(26.04.2018. law version, which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

Section 79.5 Travel for the Purpose of Terrorism

For travel for the purpose of carrying out or facilitating terrorism, joining a terrorist group or training or being trained for terrorism —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(26.04.2018. law version, which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

Section 79.6 Justification of Terrorism, Incitement to Terrorism and Terrorist Threats

(1)	For public glorification or justification of terrorism or for public incitement to terrorism, or for distribution of material glorifying, justifying or inciting terrorism —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For threats to carry out terrorism, if there is reason to believe that it may be carried out, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(26.04.2018. law version with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

Chapter X Crimes Against the State

Section 80. Activity Directed Against the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For activity directed against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power or state order of the Republic of Latvia in a manner not provided for in the Constitution of the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For the same activity, if it has been committed using violence, or if it has been committed by an organised group, or if it has been committed by a state official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(21.04.2016. law version with amendments made by the law of 14.12.2023, which comes into force on 30.12.2023.)

Section 80.1 Association in an Organised Group with the Aim of Carrying Out Activity Directed Against the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For association of more than two persons in an organised group for the purpose of acting against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power or state order of the Republic of Latvia in a manner not provided for in the Constitution of the Republic of Latvia —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For the same activity, if it has been committed by a state official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(21.04.2016. law version with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020 and 14.12.2023, which comes into force on 30.12.2023.)

81.pants. Call Directed Against the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For a public call to act against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power or state order of the Republic of Latvia in a manner not provided for in the Constitution of the Republic of Latvia, or for the distribution of material containing such a call —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.

(2)	For the same act, if committed by a group of persons or by a state official, or by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(21.04.2016. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. un 14.12.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

81.	1 pants. Assistance to a Foreign State in Activities Directed Against the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For an act with the purpose of assisting a foreigner, a foreign state or a foreign organisation to act against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power, state order or state security of the Republic of Latvia —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the same act, if committed by a group of persons or by a state official, or by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(21.04.2016. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. un 14.12.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

82. pants. Call to Liquidate the State Independence of the Republic of Latvia

(Izslēgts ar 21.04.2016. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 11.05.2016.)

83. pants. Call to Destroy the Territorial Integrity of the Republic of Latvia

(Izslēgts ar 21.04.2016. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 11.05.2016.)

84. pants

Violation of Sanctions Imposed by International Organisations and the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For violation of sanctions imposed by the United Nations, the European Union and other international organisations or for violation of national sanctions imposed by the Republic of Latvia —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed by a state official or by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 01.06.2000., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 28.01.2016., 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. un
14.12.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

85. pants. Espionage

(1)	For unlawful collection of non-disclosable information with the purpose of transmitting it or for the transmission thereof to a foreign state or a foreign organisation directly or through the intermediary of another person, or for unlawful collection or transmission of other information to a foreign intelligence service on its assignment directly or through the intermediary of another person —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of one to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For unlawful collection or transmission of a state secret to a foreign state or a foreign organisation directly or through the intermediary of another person —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(21.04.2016. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 14.12.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

85.	1 pants. Organisation and Management of Espionage

For organisation or management of espionage —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(14.12.2023. likuma redakcijā, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

86. pants

Endangering the Life and Health of the President of the Republic of Latvia, a Member of the Saeima, a Member of the Cabinet of Ministers and Other State Officials

For an attack on the President of the Republic of Latvia, a member of the Saeima, a member of the Cabinet of Ministers or another state official elected, appointed or confirmed by the Saeima of the Republic of Latvia in connection with their state activities in the interests of the Republic of Latvia, if the attack is associated with endangering the life or health of that person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012. un 14.12.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

87. pants. Endangering the Life and Health of Foreign Representatives

(1)	For an attack on the head of a foreign state or its government or another foreign representative who has officially arrived in the Republic of Latvia, if the attack is associated with endangering the life or health of that person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused serious consequences for the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012. un 14.12.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

88. pants. Terrorism

(Izslēgts ar 26.04.2018. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 23.05.2018. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 22.punktu)
