(Izslēgts ar 26.04.2018. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 23.05.2018. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 22.punktu)

88.3 pants. Recruitment and Training of Persons for the Commission of Terrorist Acts
(Izslēgts ar 26.04.2018. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 23.05.2018. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 22.punktu)

89.pants. Sabotage

For an act or omission aimed at undermining the financial system, industry, transport, agriculture, trade or other sectors of the national economy, as well as the activities of institutions or organisations, with the intent to harm the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(With amendments made by the laws of 11.12.2003. and 13.12.2012., which enter into force on 01.04.2013.)

89.	1 pants. Criminal organisation

(1)	For the establishment of a criminal organisation (association) consisting of at least five persons, with the intent to commit particularly serious crimes against humanity or peace, war crimes, to carry out genocide or to commit particularly serious crimes against the state, as well as for involvement in such an organisation or in an organised group or other criminal formation forming part of it —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to seventeen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2)	For leading a criminal organisation or participating in the crimes provided for in the first paragraph of this Section committed by such an organisation —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision paragraph 16)

90. pants

Obstruction of the exercise of electoral rights, the right to participate in the initiation of a law, the initiation of a referendum, a referendum and the support of a European Citizens' Initiative

(1)	For deliberately obstructing a person from freely participating in the collection of signatures for the initiation of a law or a referendum or for the support of a European Citizens' Initiative organised in accordance with the laws of the Republic of Latvia, by using violence, fraud, threats, bribery or in any other unlawful manner, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For deliberately obstructing a person from freely exercising the right to elect deputies and to be elected or from freely participating in a referendum organised in accordance with the laws of the Republic of Latvia, by using violence, fraud, threats, bribery or in any other unlawful manner, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 14.12.2023., which enters into force on 30.12.2023.)

91. pants. Dissemination of deliberately false information about a candidate for deputy

(Deleted by the law of 12.06.2003., which enters into force on 15.07.2003.)

92. pants

Falsification of signature collection, election and referendum documents, incorrect counting of votes and violation of secret ballot

For the falsification of signature collection documents for the initiation of a law or a referendum or for the support of a European Citizens' Initiative or for the falsification of election or referendum documents or deliberately incorrect counting of votes, as well as for deliberate violation of secret ballot, committed by a state official or a member of an election commission, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

93. pants. Desecration of state symbols

For tearing down, tearing, breaking, destroying or otherwise desecrating the coat of arms of the Latvian state or the flag of the Latvian state, as well as for public desecration of the national anthem of Latvia —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

94. pants

Deliberate disclosure of confidential, secret and top secret state secrets

For deliberate disclosure of a confidential, secret or top secret state secret, if committed by a person who has been warned against the disclosure of confidential, secret and top secret state secrets, and if this act does not have the characteristics of espionage, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enters into force on 01.05.2023.)

95. pants

Disclosure of confidential, secret and top secret state secrets through negligence

For the disclosure of a confidential, secret or top secret state secret through negligence, if committed by a person who has been warned against the disclosure of confidential, secret and top secret state secrets, or for the loss of an object containing a confidential, secret or top secret state secret, if committed by a person to whom this

object containing a state secret was entrusted, and if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enters into force on 01.05.2023.)

95.1 pants. Service abroad

For violation of the prohibition established in regulatory enactments to serve in the armed forces, internal military forces, military organisation, intelligence or security service, police (militia) or judicial institution service of a foreign state or another subject of international law or established in its territory —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 15.12.2016. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

95.	2 pants. Violation of the prohibition to organise and conduct training in the performance of military tactical tasks and to participate in it

For violation of the prohibition established in regulatory enactments to organise or conduct training in the performance of military tactical tasks, as well as for violation of the prohibition to participate in such training —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 14.12.2023., which enters into force on 30.12.2023.)

XI	nodaļa
Criminal offences against the natural environment

96. pants

Violation of the rules for the management and use of land, its subsoil, waters and forests

For violation of the rules for the management or use of land, its subsoil, waters or forests, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

97. pants. Violation of Regulations on the Use of Marine Natural Resources

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For violation of regulations on the exploration or use of natural resources in the territorial sea, continental shelf or exclusive economic zone of the Republic of Latvia, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which
enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

98. pants. Violation of Safety Regulations for the Circulation of Radioactive and Chemical Substances

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For violation of regulations on the production, acquisition, movement, storage, processing or use of radioactive substances or other sources of ionising radiation, dangerous biological, bacteriological, toxic, chemical substances or mixtures or other dangerous substances or materials, preparations or viruses, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For violation of the regulations referred to in paragraph two of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(4)	For violation of the regulations referred to in paragraph two of this Section, if it has caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 12.10.2006., 13.12.2007., 21.10.2010., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012.,
25.09.2014., 08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

98.	1 pants. Unlawful Activities with Ozone-Depleting Substances

(1)	For violation of regulations on the production, acquisition, sale, movement, storage, processing or use of ozone-depleting substances —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same activities, if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

99. pants. Violation of Waste Management Regulations

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For violation of waste management regulations, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the import of waste into the territory of Latvia or transit transportation through the territory of Latvia in violation of regulations, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(4)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two or three of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

100. pants. Unauthorised Disposal of Dangerous Substances in Waters and Underground

(1)	For unauthorised disposal of radioactive substances, including nuclear materials, dangerous chemical substances or products, materials or waste

in waters or underground —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For disposal of nuclear, chemical, biological, bacteriological, toxic or other weapons of mass destruction in waters or underground —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twenty years.
(In the wording of the law of 18.05.2000. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

101. pants. Marine Pollution

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For marine pollution with dangerous or other harmful substances, materials or waste, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For marine pollution with dangerous or other harmful substances, materials or waste, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.

102. pants. Pollution and Littering of Land, Forests and Waters

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For pollution of land, forests or inland waters (surface or underground) with dangerous or other harmful substances, materials or waste, littering or other harmful impact on them in any way, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For pollution of land, forests or inland waters (surface or underground) with dangerous or other harmful substances, materials or waste, littering or other harmful impact on them in any way, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.

which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

103. pants. Air Pollution

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For air pollution, littering, physical or other harmful impact on it in any way, exceeding established standards or violating regulations, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022.
which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

104. Section. Operation of Facilities Without Treatment Structures

For the operation of industrial, agricultural, municipal or other facilities if the necessary treatment, harmful substance and dust capture structures and devices have not been installed therein or they are in such a condition that they are not fit for operation, and if significant harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the version of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which come into force on 04.05.2022.)

104. 1 Section. Unlawful Operation of Installations

For unlawful operation of an installation specified in a pollution-regulating regulatory enactment in which environmentally hazardous activities are carried out or hazardous substances or preparations are stored or used, if significant harm has been caused thereby,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the version of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which come into force on 04.05.2022.)

105. Section. Failure to Take Measures to Eliminate Environmental Pollution

For failure to take or improper taking of measures necessary to eliminate environmental pollution and other harmful consequences that are included in the duties of a person, as well as for failure to report if harmful consequences have occurred,
—

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

106. Section. Concealment of Data on Environmental Pollution

(1)	For failure to report pollution of sea waters or other harmful impact from vehicles or structures at sea, if committed by a person who had a duty to report, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For intentional concealment or falsification of data on the extent of environmental pollution, if committed by a person who had a duty to provide such data, and if significant harm has been caused as a result thereof, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the version of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which come into force on 04.05.2022.)

107. Section. Forest Burning

(1)	For intentional setting fire to a forest —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if significant harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For the same acts, if they have caused the death of a person or other serious consequences through the fault of the guilty person due to negligence,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the version of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

108. Section. Destruction and Damage of Forest by Negligence

(1)	For destruction or damage of a forest area by negligence, through careless handling of fire or in another manner, if significant harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused the death of a person or other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

109. Section. Unlawful Felling and Damage of Trees

(1)	For unlawful felling of trees in another person's forest or other land area belonging to another person —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For unlawful felling, destruction or damage of trees in a specially protected nature area, micro-reserve, park, square, avenue or environmental and natural resource protection buffer zone —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For unlawful felling, destruction or damage of trees, if significant harm has been caused by such acts, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(4)	For the criminal offence provided for in the first, second or third paragraph of this Section, if committed by an organised group,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(In the version of the law of 13.12.2007. with amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017. and
17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

110. Section. Unlawful Fishing and Acquisition of Aquatic Animals

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For catching or otherwise acquiring fish or aquatic animals without the relevant permit, at a prohibited time or in prohibited places, or with prohibited equipment or methods (unlawful acquisition), if significant harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(21) For the criminal offence provided for in the second paragraph of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed in a specially protected nature area, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For unlawful acquisition of fish or aquatic animals using electric current, explosive substances, poisonous substances or other generally dangerous means or methods, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which
come into force on 01.01.2022.)

111. Section

Unlawful	Manufacture,	Acquisition,	Storage,	Sale, Transportation and Dispatch of Electrofishing Devices

For unlawful manufacture, acquisition, storage, sale, transportation or dispatch of electrofishing devices —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

112. Section. Illegal Hunting

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For illegal hunting, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if it has been committed in a specially protected nature territory, or if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the use of prohibited generally dangerous means, methods, tools or techniques in hunting —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by the law of 18.12.2003. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

113. Section

Performance of Blasting and Other Works in Violation of Animal Protection Regulations

For the performance of blasting, melioration, timber harvesting or other works in violation of animal protection regulations, if such activities have caused substantial harm to fish resources, birds or other wild animals, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

114. Section. Destruction and Damage of Specially Protected Nature Territories

For the destruction or damage of specially protected nature territories, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on

01.01.2022.)

115. Section. Destruction and Damage of Specially Protected Animals and Plants

For the catching, keeping or destruction of specially protected animals or for the acquisition, keeping, damage or destruction of specially protected plants, fungi or lichens, or for the destruction or damage of habitats of specially protected species or specially protected biotopes, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

115. 1 Section. Violation of Trade Regulations for Specimens of Endangered Wild Fauna and Flora Species

For violation of trade regulations for specimens of endangered wild fauna or flora species or their parts or products, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

XII Chapter Killing

116. Section. Murder

For intentional unlawful killing of another person (murder) —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 15.05.2014., which comes into force on 14.06.2014. See transitional provision paragraph 11)

117. Section. Murder in Aggravating Circumstances

For murder, if:

1)	a woman has been murdered, the guilty person being aware that she is in a state of pregnancy;

2)	a person has been murdered, the guilty person being aware that it is in a helpless state;

3)	it has been committed in a manner dangerous to the life of several persons;

4)	it has been committed with particular cruelty;

5)	desecration of the corpse has been committed after it;

6)	it is connected with robbery;

7)	it is connected with rape;

8)	it has been committed with the intent to conceal another criminal offence or to facilitate its commission;

9)	it has been committed with mercenary intent;

10)	it has been committed by a group of persons;

11)	it has been committed by a person placed in a place of short-term detention or imprisonment;

12)	a minor has been murdered,—

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from ten to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(With amendments made by the laws of 18.05.2000., 08.07.2011. and 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision paragraph 11)

118. Section. Murder in Particularly Aggravating Circumstances

For murder

1)	in connection with the fact that the victim or his or her relative has performed his or her official or professional duties or participated in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;

2)	if two or more persons have been murdered;

3)	(deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.);

4)	if it has been committed by a person serving life imprisonment;

5)	if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from fifteen to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 01.12.2011. and 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision paragraph 11)

119. Section. Murder of a Newborn Child

For the murder of her child, committed by the mother during childbirth or immediately after childbirth under the influence of the mental and physiological state caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

120. Section. Murder Committed in a State of Strong Mental Agitation

For murder committed in a state of sudden strong mental agitation caused by violence or serious insult to honour on the part of the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

121. Section. Murder in Violation of the Limits of Necessary Defence

For murder committed in violation of the limits of necessary defence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

122. Section. Murder in Violation of the Conditions for Apprehension of a Person

(1)	For murder committed in violation of the conditions for apprehension of a person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by a State official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

123. pants. Manslaughter by Negligence

(1)	For unlawful manslaughter by negligence —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(2)	For unlawful manslaughter by negligence, if two or more persons have been killed or if the manslaughter was committed while handling a firearm or explosives or in another generally dangerous manner, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

124. pants. Driving to Suicide

(1)	For driving a person to suicide or an attempt thereof, by treating the victim cruelly or systematically humiliating his or her personal dignity, if such person was not in material or other dependence on the guilty person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.
(2)	For the same acts in relation to a person who was in material or other dependence on the guilty person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation
supervision, or by community service.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

XIII	nodaļa
Criminal Offences against the Health of a Person

125. pants. Intentional Grievous Bodily Harm

(1)	For intentional infliction of grievous bodily harm —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2)	For the same acts, if:

1)	they were committed in connection with the fact that the victim or his or her relative has performed his or her official or professional duties or participated in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;

2)	they were committed against two or more persons;

3)	they were committed in a manner dangerous to the life or health of several persons;

4)	they were of a torturing or torture nature;

5)	they were committed by a group of persons;

6)	(deleted by the law of 13.12.2012);

7)	they were committed by a person placed in a place of short-term detention or imprisonment;

8)	they were committed against a person in a helpless condition;

9)	they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is in the first or second degree of kinship, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For infliction of intentional grievous bodily harm which, due to the negligence of the guilty person, has been the cause of the victim's death, or for infliction of intentional grievous bodily harm, if it was committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(4)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012)
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007, 30.10.2008, 21.05.2009, 08.07.2011, 13.12.2012, 08.06.2017 and
26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

126. pants. Intentional Bodily Harm of Medium Gravity

(1)	For intentional infliction of bodily harm of medium gravity —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if:

1)	they were committed in connection with the fact that the victim or his or her relative has performed his or her official or professional duties or participated in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;

2)	they were of a torturing or torture nature;

3)	they were committed by a group of persons;

4)	(deleted by the law of 13.12.2012);

5)	they were committed by a person placed in a place of short-term detention or imprisonment;

6)	they were committed against a person in a helpless condition;

7)	they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is in the first or second degree of kinship,

or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 30.10.2008, 21.05.2009, 13.12.2012, 08.06.2017, 26.04.2018 and 17.12.2020,
which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

127. pants. Intentional Bodily Harm Inflicted in a State of Strong Mental Agitation

For intentional grievous or medium gravity bodily harm inflicted in a state of sudden strong mental agitation caused by violence or serious insult to honour from the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

128. pants

Infliction of Intentional Bodily Harm by Exceeding the Limits of Necessary Defence

For infliction of intentional grievous or medium gravity bodily harm by exceeding the limits of necessary defence, if such harm was not inflicted in defence against a threat to life or against rape, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

129. pants

Infliction of Intentional Bodily Harm by Violating the Conditions of Detention of a Person

(1)	For infliction of intentional grievous or medium gravity bodily harm by violating the conditions of detention of a person, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they were committed by a state official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004, 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

130. pants. Intentional Minor Bodily Injury

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2)	For intentional infliction of minor bodily injury, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the same acts, if:

1)	they were committed in connection with the victim or their relative having performed their official or professional duties or having participated in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or having given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;

2)	they were of the nature of torture or torment;

3)	they were committed by a group of persons;

4)	they were committed by a person placed in a place of short-term detention or imprisonment;

5)	they were committed against a person in a helpless state;

6)	they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is in the first or second degree of kinship, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 08.06.2017., 26.04.2018. and 17.12.2020.
which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

130. 1 pants. Torture

For torture, if these acts have not had the consequences provided for in Articles 125, 126 or 130 of this law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

131. pants. Bodily Injury by Negligence

For infliction of serious or medium gravity bodily injury by negligence —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

132. pants. Threats to Commit Murder and to Inflict Serious Bodily Injury

(1)	For threats to commit murder or to inflict serious bodily injury, if there has been reason to fear that these threats may be carried out, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is in the first or second degree of kinship, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 15.06.2023., which enters into force on 15.07.2023. See transitional provision point 25.1)

132.1 pants. Stalking

(1)	For repeated or prolonged tracking, surveillance of another person, making threats to that person or unwanted communication with that person, if that person has had reason to fear for their own or their relatives' safety, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is in the first or second degree of kinship, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence

is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 08.06.2017. with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 15.06.2023., which enters into force on 15.07.2023. See transitional provision point 25.1)

133.pants. Infection with Human Immunodeficiency and Hepatitis B and C Virus

For deliberate infection of a person with human immunodeficiency virus or hepatitis B or C virus —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

133. 1 pants. Infection with a Dangerous Infectious Disease Pathogen

For deliberate infection of a person with a dangerous infectious disease pathogen, if as a result serious consequences have been caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

134. pants. Infection with a Sexually Transmitted Disease Pathogen

(1)	For deliberate infection of a person with a sexually transmitted disease pathogen —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For deliberate infection of a minor with a sexually transmitted disease pathogen —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

135. pants. Unlawful Performance of Abortion

(1)	For an abortion performed on a pregnant woman by a person who has the right to do so, if the abortion was performed outside a hospital or other medical institution or in a medical institution but without legal basis, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term of up to five years.

(2)	For performing an abortion on a pregnant woman in unsanitary conditions or if it was performed by a person who does not have the right to perform an abortion,—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term of up to five years.

(3)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(4)	For unlawful performance of an abortion against the will of the pregnant woman or if the unlawful performance of an abortion caused the death of the pregnant woman or other serious consequences —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

136. pants. Coercion to Have an Abortion

For coercion of a pregnant woman to have an abortion, if as a result an abortion was performed, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

137. pants. Unlawful Medical Practice

(1)	For unlawful medical practice, if it has caused a health disorder to the victim, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For unlawful medical practice, if it has caused the death of the victim or serious bodily injury through the negligence of the guilty person,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

138. pants. Improper Performance of Professional Duties by a Medical Practitioner

(1)	For failure to perform professional duties by a medical practitioner or for negligent performance thereof, if this act has caused serious or moderate bodily injury to the victim through the negligence of the guilty person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same act, if it has caused infection of the victim with human immunodeficiency virus or hepatitis B or C virus, or has been the cause of the death of the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

139. pants. Unlawful Removal and Use of Human Tissues and Organs

(1)	For unlawful removal or use of tissues or organs of a deceased person for medical or any other purposes —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For unlawful removal or use of tissues or organs of a living person for medical or any other purposes —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they have been committed by a group of persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.

(4)	For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have been committed by a medical practitioner or if they have been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 06.07.2021., which enter into force on 05.08.2021. The amendment in the first paragraph replacing the words "forced labour" with the words "probation supervision, or by community service" enters into force on 01.01.2022. The amendment in the second paragraph replacing the words "or by forced labour" with the words "or by probation supervision, or by community service" enters into force on 01.01.2022. See transitional provision point 26)

139.	1 pants. Recruitment of Donors and Recipients of Human Tissues and Organs
(1)	For recruitment of a donor or recipient for unlawful removal or transplantation of human tissues or organs — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation
supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they have been committed by a group of persons, or if they have been committed by a medical practitioner, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 06.06.2019. with amendments made by the law of 06.07.2021., which enters into force on 05.08.2021. The amendments in the first and second paragraphs replacing the words "or by forced labour" with the words "or by probation supervision, or by community service" enter into force on 01.01.2022. See transitional provision point 26)

140. pants. Violation of Sanitary-Hygienic and Epidemiological Safety Regulations

For violation of sanitary-hygienic or epidemiological safety regulations, if it has caused an epidemic or other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

141. pants. Abandonment Without Assistance

(1)	For failure to provide necessary and obviously urgent assistance to a person who is in a life-threatening condition, if the guilty person was aware that he or she could provide it without serious danger to himself or herself and other persons, and if the failure to provide assistance has caused the death of a person or other serious consequences,—

shall be punished by probation supervision or by community service or by a fine.

(2)	For deliberate abandonment without assistance of a person who is in a condition dangerous to life or health and who has no possibility to preserve himself or herself due to minority, age, illness or his or her incapacity, if the guilty person had the possibility to provide assistance to the victim and had a duty to care for him or her or the guilty person himself or herself placed him or her in a life-threatening condition, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(21) For the acts provided for in the second paragraph of this Section, if the failure to provide assistance has caused the death of a person or other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation

supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if the failure to provide assistance has caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 07.01.2021. and 17.12.2020. and 06.07.2021., which enter into force on 05.08.2021. The amendment in paragraph 2.1 replacing the words "or by forced labour" with the words "or by probation supervision, or by community service" enters into force on 01.01.2022. See transitional provision point 26)

142. pants. Failure to Provide Assistance to Victims at Sea

(1)	For failure to proceed to the scene of distress at sea, if a message has been received that assistance is necessary, and if committed by a ship's captain, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For failure to provide assistance to persons who are perishing at sea or on another waterway, if this assistance could be provided without serious danger to the ship, its crew and passengers and if committed by a ship's captain, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

XIV	nodaļa
Criminal Offences Against Fundamental Human Rights and Freedoms

143. Section. Violation of the Inviolability of a Person's Dwelling

(1)	For unlawful entry into a dwelling against the will of the person residing therein —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, using violence, threats, fraud or impersonating a state official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 20.03.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

144. Section

Violation of the Secrecy of Correspondence and Information Transmitted via Electronic Communications Networks

(1)	For intentional violation of the secrecy of a person's correspondence —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For unlawful interception of publicly unavailable data transmission or signals in electronic communications networks, as well as for unlawful acquisition of publicly unavailable electromagnetic data from an electronic communications network in which such data is located, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

145. Section. Unlawful Actions with Personal Data of a Natural Person

(1)	For unlawful actions with personal data of a natural person, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For unlawful actions with personal data of a natural person, if committed by a personal data controller or processor with the intent of revenge, mercenary intent or blackmail, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For influencing a personal data controller or processor or a data subject by using violence or threats or by abusing trust, or by fraud with the intent to commit unlawful actions with personal data of a natural person
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 10.09.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

146. Section. Violation of Occupational Safety Regulations

(1)	For violation of the requirements of regulatory enactments regulating occupational safety or technical safety, if committed by the manager of an enterprise (company), institution or organisation or another person responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if this offence has caused bodily injury with health impairment or permanent loss of capacity to work, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(2)	For the same offence, if it has caused the death of a person or serious bodily injury to several persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation
supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

147. Section. Violation of the Rights of an Inventor and Designer

(1)	For intentional disclosure of an invention or design without the permission of the inventor, designer or their successor in title, before the invention or design has been disclosed by the relevant person themselves or before it has been disclosed with the consent of that person, as well as for appropriation of authorship of an invention or design or imposition of co-authorship —

shall be punished by probation supervision or community service or a fine.

(2)	For coercion by violence or threats thereof, or by blackmail to renounce authorship of an invention or design or for imposition of co-authorship —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which

enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

148. Section. Violation of Copyright and Related Rights

(1)	For violation of copyright or related rights, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For violation of copyright or related rights, if committed on a large scale, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by an organised group, or for coercion by violence, threats or blackmail to renounce authorship, or for imposition of co-authorship, if committed by violence, threats or blackmail,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the wording of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and
07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

149. Section. Unlawful Actions with Copyright and Related Rights Objects

(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010., which enters into force on 01.01.2011.)

149. 1 Section. Violation of the Prohibition of Discrimination

(1)	For discrimination on the grounds of racial, national, ethnic or religious affiliation or for violation of the prohibition of other types of discrimination, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a state official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, or by a group of persons or if committed using an automated data processing system, or for the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if associated with torture, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

150. Section. Incitement of Social Hatred and Discord

(1)	For actions aimed at inciting hatred or discord based on a person's gender, age, disability or any other characteristics, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by a State official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, or by a group of persons or if it has been committed using an automated data processing system, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are associated with violence or threats, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As amended by the Law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the Laws of 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

151. Section. Disruption of Religious Rituals

(Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

XV	Chapter
Criminal Offences against Personal Liberty, Honour and Dignity

152. Section. Unlawful Deprivation of Liberty

(1)	For unlawful actions depriving a person of the possibility to freely determine their location (unlawful deprivation of liberty), if there are no elements of a criminal offence by a State official, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same actions, if they have been committed in a manner dangerous to the victim's life or health or if they are associated with infliction of physical suffering on them, or if they have lasted more than one week, or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For unlawful deprivation of liberty, if it has caused serious consequences or if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which
enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

153. Section. Abduction of a Person

(1)	For seizure of a person using violence or threats, or taking away by deceit or by exploiting the person's state of helplessness (abduction of a person), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same actions, if they have been committed against a minor, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3)	For abduction of a person, if it has caused serious consequences or it has been committed against an infant, or it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2007., 30.10.2008., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which
enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

154. Section. Taking of Hostages

(1)	For seizure or detention of a person as a hostage, if it is associated with threats to kill, inflict bodily injury or continue to detain this person with the aim of forcing a natural or legal person or a group of persons to perform any action or refrain from it, setting it as a condition for the release of the hostage, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2)	For the same actions, if they have been committed against a minor or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3)	For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences or if they have been committed against an infant, or if they have been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 08.12.2005., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011. and 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision paragraph 11)

154.1 Section. Trafficking in Human Beings

(1)	For trafficking in human beings —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For trafficking in human beings, if it has been committed against a minor or if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For trafficking in human beings, if it has endangered the victim's life or caused serious consequences or if it has been committed with particular cruelty or against an infant, or if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(As amended by the Law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the Laws of 16.12.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 14.03.2013., which enters into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision paragraph 11)

154.	2 Section. Concept of Trafficking in Human Beings

(1)	Trafficking in human beings is the recruitment, transportation, transfer, harbouring, accommodation or receipt of persons for the purpose of exploitation, using violence or threats, or taking away by deceit or by exploiting the person's dependence on the guilty party or their vulnerability or state of helplessness, or by giving or receiving material or other benefits in order to obtain the consent to trafficking of the person on whom the victim is dependent.

(2)	The recruitment, transportation, transfer, harbouring, accommodation or receipt of a minor for the purpose of exploitation shall be recognised as trafficking in human beings even if it is not associated with the use of any of the means referred to in paragraph one of this Section.

(3)	Exploitation within the meaning of this Section is the involvement of a person in prostitution or other forms of sexual exploitation, forcing to perform work, provide services or commit criminal offences, keeping in slavery or other similar forms (debt bondage, serfdom or other forms of forced transfer of a person into dependence on another person), keeping in servitude or unlawful removal of a person's tissues or organs.

(4)	State of vulnerability within the meaning of this Section means that circumstances are exploited where the person has no other real or acceptable choice but to submit to exploitation.
(As amended by the Law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 25.09.2014., which enters into force on 29.10.2014.)

155. pants. Unlawful Placement in a Psychiatric Hospital

For deliberately unlawful placement of a person in a psychiatric hospital —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a period of up to five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

156. pants. Defamation of Honour

(Deleted by the law of 19.11.2009., which enters into force on 23.12.2009.)

157. pants. Slander

(1)	For deliberate dissemination of knowingly false fabrications defaming another person in a printed or otherwise reproduced work, as well as orally, if committed publicly (slander), —

shall be punished by probation supervision or by community service or by a fine.

(2)	For slander in a mass medium —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 12.06.2003. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

158. pants. Defamation of Honour and Slander in a Mass Medium

(Deleted by the law of 19.11.2009., which enters into force on 23.12.2009.)

XVI	nodaļa Criminal Offences against
Morality and Sexual Inviolability

159. pants. Rape

(1)	For sexual intercourse by exploiting the victim's helpless condition or for sexual intercourse against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim (rape), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of four to ten years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.

(2)	For rape, if committed by a group of persons, or for rape of a minor —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to twenty years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.

(3)	For rape, if it has caused serious consequences, or for rape of a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the law of 12.11.2015., which enters into force on 02.12.2015.)

160. pants. Sexual Violence

(1)	For an act of a sexual nature for the purpose of satisfying one's sexual desire in physical contact with the victim's body, if committed by exploiting the victim's helpless condition or against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.

(2)	For anal or oral intercourse or satisfaction of sexual desire in an unnatural manner involving vaginal, anal or oral penetration of the victim's body, if committed by exploiting the victim's helpless condition or against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of four to ten years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons or if committed with a minor, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.

(4)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences or if committed with a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to fifteen years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.

(5)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons or if committed with a minor, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to twenty years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.

(6)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences or if committed with a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, —

shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 25.09.2014. and 12.11.2015., which enters into force on 02.12.2015.)

161. pants

Acts of a Sexual Nature with a Person Who Has Not Reached the Age of Sixteen

For committing sexual intercourse, anal or oral intercourse or satisfaction of sexual desire in an unnatural manner, or committing other acts of a sexual nature in physical contact with the victim's body, if committed with a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, and committed by an adult, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.
(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 12.11.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

162. pants. Seduction into Debauchery

(1)	For seduction into debauchery of a person who has not reached the age of sixteen or a person in a helpless condition, that is, for committing an act of a sexual nature without physical contact with the victim's body for the purpose of satisfying one's sexual desire or arousing sexual instinct in the victim, if committed by an adult or if committed by exploiting the victim's helpless condition or against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences or if committed with an infant, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and by probation supervision for a period of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 12.11.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

162.1 pants. Incitement to Engage in Sexual Activities

(1)	For inciting a person who has not reached the age of sixteen to engage in sexual activities or inciting such a person to meet for the purpose of committing sexual acts or entering into sexual relations, using information or communication technologies or other means of communication, if committed by an adult, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed with an infant, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.
(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 12.11.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

163.pants. Violation of Provisions Restricting Prostitution

(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

163.	1 pants. Establishment, Maintenance, Management and Financing of a Brothel
