For establishment, maintenance, management or financing of a brothel (an unauthorised place for organising and providing prostitution services) —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

164. Section. Involvement of Persons in Prostitution and Use of Prostitution

(1)	For involvement of a person in prostitution —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For coercion to engage in prostitution or for involvement of a person in prostitution by abusing their trust or by fraud, or by exploiting the person's dependence on the guilty party or their state of helplessness, or for deliberate use of prostitution of a victim of human trafficking —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation

supervision, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the acts provided for in the first or second part of this Section, if committed by a group of persons, or for inciting, involving or coercing a minor to engage in prostitution, or for providing premises to minors for prostitution —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(31) For use of prostitution of a minor —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.

(4)	For inciting, involving or coercing a person who has not reached the age of sixteen to engage in prostitution —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(5)	For the acts provided for in this Section, if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 18.05.2000., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012.,
15.05.2014., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

165. Section. Pimping

(1)	For exploitation of a person who engages in prostitution for the purpose of subsistence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons or if committed in respect of a minor, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3)	For the same acts, if committed by an organised group or if committed in respect of a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 18.05.2000., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. and
17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

165.1 Section. Sending of a Person for Sexual Exploitation

(1)	For sending a person with their consent for sexual exploitation, that is, for any action which for this purpose facilitates legal or illegal movement, transit or stay of a person in the territory of one country or several countries,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed for the purpose of subsistence or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For the acts provided for in the first or second part of this Section, if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 18.05.2000. with amendments made by the laws of 16.12.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011.,
13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

165.	2 Section. Sending Abroad
(Deleted by the law of 21.05.2009., which enters into force on 01.07.2009.)

166. Section

Violation of Restrictions on Demonstration of Pornographic Performance, Entertainment of an Intimate Nature and Rules on Circulation of Pornographic Material

(1)	For violation of restrictions on demonstration of a pornographic performance or other entertainment of an intimate nature or for violation of rules on circulation of pornographic material, if committed on a significant scale or if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For attendance or demonstration of such a pornographic performance or for circulation of such pornographic material which contains child pornography, sexual activity of a human being with an animal, necrophilia or satisfaction of sexual desire in a violent manner, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For inciting, involving, coercing a minor to participate or using a minor in a pornographic performance or in the production of pornographic material —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(4)	For inciting, involving, coercing a person who has not reached the age of sixteen to participate or using such person in a pornographic performance or in the production of pornographic material —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(5)	For the acts provided for in the third or fourth part of this Section, if committed by an organised group or committed using violence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

XVII	Chapter Criminal Offences against the Family and Minors

167. Section. Substitution of a Child

(1)	For deliberate substitution of a newborn child —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For substitution of a newborn child for a mercenary purpose —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

168. pants

Failure to Comply with a Decision on Custody, Care and Contact Rights with a Child

For evasion of the execution of a court or orphan's court decision arising from custody, care or contact rights with a child, as well as a court decision providing for the return of a child to the country of his or her residence, or malicious non-compliance with such a decision, or malicious obstruction of the execution of such a decision —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(As worded by the Law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and
17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

168. 1 pants. Failure to Comply with a Decision on Protection against Violence

For malicious non-compliance with a decision on protection against violence —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(As worded by the Law of 20.03.2014. with amendments made by the Laws of 29.10.2015., 17.12.2020. and 15.06.2023., which comes into force on 15.07.2023. See transitional provision point 25.1)

169. pants. Disclosure of Adoption Secret

For disclosure of an adoption secret against the will of the adoptee —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

169. 1 pants. Unlawful Actions in Arranging Adoption

(1) For giving consent to the adoption of a minor, if given by the mother, father or guardian of that minor with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For requesting consent to the adoption of a minor from the mother, father or guardian of that minor personally or through an intermediary, using violence, threats, fraud, bribery or other unlawful means, as well as for such intermediation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the actions provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(As worded by the Law of 21.06.2007. with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

170. pants. Evasion of Maintenance

For evasion of compliance with a court decision or decision of a competent state institution imposing an obligation to support and provide maintenance to one's parents, grandparents, children, grandchildren or other persons, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which
comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

171. pants. Malicious Abuse of Guardian's Rights

For the use of guardianship or curatorship to the detriment of the ward or person under curatorship, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

172. pants. Involvement of a Minor in a Criminal Offence

For involving a minor in a criminal offence —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

173. pants

Bringing a Minor to a State of Intoxication, Involving in Non-Medical Use of Medicinal and Other Substances Causing Intoxication

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For deliberately bringing a minor to a state of intoxication or for involving a minor in the non-medical use of medicinal or other substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic substances but cause intoxication, if committed using violence or threats, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

174. pants. Cruelty and Violence against a Minor

(1) For cruel or violent treatment of a minor, if it has caused physical or mental suffering to the minor and if it has been committed by persons on whom the victim is materially or otherwise dependent and if these actions have not had the consequences provided for in Section 125 or 126 of this Law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed against an infant, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 30.10.2008., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.01.2021., which
comes into force on 02.02.2021.)

XVIII nodaļa
Criminal Offences against Property

175. pants. Theft

(1) For secret or open misappropriation of movable property belonging to another (theft) —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(2) For theft, if committed in a significant amount or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation
supervision, or community service, or a fine, with or without confiscation of property.

(3) For theft, if committed by entering a vehicle, dwelling or other premises, or if committed from a storage facility or device connecting storage facilities, as well as for theft of a vehicle —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with or without confiscation of property.

(4) For theft, if committed in a large amount or if committed by an organised group, as well as for theft of narcotic, psychotropic, potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 25.04.2002., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which
comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

176. Section. Robbery

(1) For the robbery of another's movable property, if it is connected with violence or threat of violence (robbery), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For robbery, if it has been committed in a substantial amount or if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if it has been committed by entering a vehicle, dwelling or other premises, or if it has been committed from a storage facility or a device connecting storage facilities, as well as for the robbery of a vehicle —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3) For robbery, if it has been committed in a large amount or if it has been committed by an organised group, as well as for the robbery of narcotic, psychotropic, potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(4) For robbery, if it has been committed using firearms or explosives, or if it is connected with the infliction of serious bodily injury on the victim, or if it has caused other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020.
which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

177. Section. Fraud

(1) For the acquisition of another's property or rights to such property by abusing trust or by deceit (fraud), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For fraud, if it has been committed in a substantial amount or if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For fraud, if it has been committed in a large amount or if it has been committed by an organised group, or if it has been committed by acquiring narcotic, psychotropic, potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022.
which enter into force on 04.05.2022.)

177. 1 Section. Fraud in an automated data processing system

(1) For the acquisition of another's property or rights to such property, or other material benefits, by deliberately entering false data into an automated data processing system in order to influence the operation of its resources (computer fraud), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For computer fraud, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For computer fraud, if it has been committed in a large amount or if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

178. Section. Insurance fraud

(1) For the intentional destruction, damage or concealment of one's own property with the aim of receiving insurance compensation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For coercing or persuading another person to destroy, damage or conceal insured property or other influence with the same aim, if it has been committed by the owner of the property with the aim of receiving the insurance sum, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have been committed with the aim of receiving the insurance sum in a large amount, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

179. Section. Misappropriation

(1) For the unlawful acquisition or squandering of another's property, if it has been committed by a person to whom this property was entrusted or in whose charge it was (misappropriation), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For misappropriation, if it has been committed in a substantial amount or if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For misappropriation, if it has been committed in a large amount, as well as for the misappropriation of narcotic, psychotropic, potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enter into force on 04.05.2022.)

180. Section. Theft, fraud, misappropriation in a small amount

(1) For theft, fraud or misappropriation in a small amount, except for the offences provided for in the third and fourth paragraphs of Section 175, the third paragraph of Section 177 and the third paragraph of Section 179 of this Law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)
(In the wording of the law of 15.01.2004. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

181. Section. Repeated theft, fraud, misappropriation

(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

182. pants

Unauthorised Consumption of Electricity, Thermal Energy and Gas, Unauthorised Use of Electronic Communications Services

(1)	For unauthorised consumption of electricity, thermal energy or gas or for unauthorised use of electronic communications services, if committed on a significant scale, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For unauthorised consumption of electricity, thermal energy or gas or for unauthorised use of electronic communications services, if committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(As worded by the Law of 21.06.2007. with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

182. 1 pants. Unlawful Actions with Commercial Metering of Consumed Electricity, Thermal Energy and Gas

(1)	For interference with the operation of commercial metering devices for electricity, thermal energy or gas or for disruption thereof, or for the manufacture, adaptation, distribution or installation of equipment, devices or software, if it creates the possibility for persons to consume electricity, thermal energy or gas unauthorisedly, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(As worded by the Law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

183. pants. Extortion

(1)	For a demand without legal basis to surrender property or rights to property or to perform any actions of a proprietary nature, threatening violence against the victim or his or her relatives, threatening to disclose disgraceful information about the victim or his or her relatives, threatening to destroy their property or cause them other substantial harm (extortion), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For extortion, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed using violence, weapons or explosives, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

184. pants. Extortion in an Organised Group

(1)	For the creation of an organised group or participation therein for the purpose of extortion —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For extortion as part of an organised group, if the extortion was committed using violence or threats, weapons or explosives, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011. and 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision paragraph 11)

185. pants. Intentional Destruction and Damage of Property

(1)	For intentional destruction or damage of another's property —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For intentional destruction or damage of another's property, if committed by burning or in another generally dangerous manner

or if committed on a large scale, or if as a result thereof the death of a person has occurred through the negligence of the guilty person or other serious consequences have been caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

186. pants. Destruction and Damage of Property through Negligence

(1)	For destruction or damage of another's property through negligence, acting carelessly with fire or in another generally dangerous manner and if as a result thereof substantial harm has been caused, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For destruction or damage of another's property through negligence, if as a result thereof the death of a person has occurred or other serious consequences have been caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For destruction or damage of another's property through negligence, if as a result thereof the death of two or more persons has occurred, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which
comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

187. pants

Intentional Destruction and Damage of Electricity Network, Public Electronic Communications Network, Thermal Network, Gas, Oil and Oil Product Pipeline

(1)	For intentional destruction or damage of an electricity network, public electronic communications network, thermal network, gas, oil or oil product pipeline or its installations —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if as a result thereof the death of a person has occurred or if they have caused an accident, disaster or other serious consequences, or if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2007., 21.10.2010., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which
comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

188. pants. Destruction and Damage of Gas and Oil Pipelines through Negligence

(1)	For destruction or damage of gas, oil or oil product pipelines and their installations through negligence — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(2)	For the same acts, if as a result thereof the death of a person has occurred or if they have caused an accident, disaster or other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if as a result thereof the death of two or more persons has occurred, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which
comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

189. Section. Negligent and Careless Guarding of Property

(Repealed by the law of 11.06.2020., which comes into force on 06.07.2020.)

Chapter XIX
Criminal Offences in the National Economy

190. Section. Smuggling

(1)	For the importation into the customs territory of the Republic of Latvia or exportation therefrom of goods subject to customs clearance or other valuables, bypassing customs control or concealing them from such control, or failing to declare them, or using forged customs or other documents, or in any other unlawful manner (smuggling), if committed on a substantial scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by an organised group, or for smuggling, if committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(4)	(Repealed by the law of 13.12.2012.)
(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017.,
03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

Section 190.1 Movement of Goods and Substances, the Circulation of which is Prohibited or Specially Regulated, across the State Border of the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For the movement across the state border of the Republic of Latvia in any unlawful manner of a narcotic or psychotropic substance, raw material (precursor) intended for the manufacture of such substances, a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, as well as of a radioactive or hazardous substance, goods of strategic importance or other valuables, explosives, weapons, ammunition —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the same acts, if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 21.06.2007., 13.12.2007., 19.11.2009., 08.07.2011.,
13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

191. Section. Unauthorised Actions with Goods Subject to Customs Clearance and Other Valuables

(1)	For the storage, movement, forwarding or sale in the customs territory of the Republic of Latvia of goods subject to customs clearance or other valuables without the authorisation of the customs authorities, if committed on a substantial scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the storage, movement, forwarding or sale in the customs territory of the Republic of Latvia of goods subject to customs clearance or other valuables without the authorisation of the customs authorities, if committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision or without it, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

192. Section

Manufacture, Distribution, Transportation, Forwarding, Acquisition and Storage of Counterfeit Money and State Financial Instruments

(1)	For the transportation, forwarding, acquisition or storage with the intention to distribute of counterfeit banknotes, coins, state financial instruments or foreign currency in circulation or intended for circulation in the Republic of Latvia, or for the manufacture thereof, as well as for the distribution of such counterfeits —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the manufacture of counterfeit banknotes, coins, state financial instruments or foreign currency in circulation or intended for circulation in the Republic of Latvia, or the distribution of such counterfeits, if committed on a substantial scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 10.04.2003., 18.12.2003., 05.05.2005., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012.,
28.01.2016. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

Section 192.1 Manufacture, Acquisition, Storage and Distribution of Equipment, Software, Data and Other Means Adapted for Counterfeiting Money

For the manufacture, acquisition, storage or distribution of such equipment, software, data, security (anti-counterfeiting) elements or any other means that are adapted for counterfeiting banknotes, coins, state financial instruments or foreign currency in circulation or intended for circulation in the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 28.01.2016. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

193. pants. Unlawful Actions with Financial Instruments and Means of Payment

(1)	(Deleted by law of 28.01.2016.)

(2)	For the robbery, destruction, damage or unlawful use of another person's financial instrument or means of payment, or for the acquisition, storage, transportation or distribution of a robbed means of payment —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the counterfeiting of a financial instrument or means of payment, as well as for the acquisition, storage, transportation, distribution or use of such a counterfeit, if there are no elements of the crime provided for in Section 192 of this Law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4)	For the actions provided for in paragraph two or three of this Section, if they have been committed on a large scale or if they have been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(18.12.2003. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015.,
28.01.2016., 06.07.2021. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

193.1 pants. Acquisition, Production, Distribution, Use and Storage of Data, Software and Equipment for Unlawful Actions with Financial Instruments and Means of Payment

(1)	For the acquisition, transportation or distribution of data that enables the unlawful use of a financial instrument or means of payment, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the use of data that enables the unlawful use of a financial instrument or means of payment, or for the production or adaptation of software or equipment for the commission of crimes provided for in Section 193 of this Law, as well as for the acquisition, transportation, storage or distribution of such software or equipment for the same purpose —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the actions provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(01.06.2000. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 18.12.2003., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012.,
06.07.2021. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

193.2 pants. Unlawful Use of Inside Information and Market Manipulation in Financial Markets

(1)	For the unlawful use of inside information in financial markets, recommending to another person or inducing another person to engage in the unlawful use of inside information in financial markets, as well as for market manipulation in financial markets, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(28.01.2016. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

194.pants. Unauthorised Issue of Financial Instruments

(1)	For the placing in circulation of financial instruments of a legal person before the legal person has commenced operations or without the registration of financial instruments required by law, or for the provision of knowingly false information regarding the placing of financial instruments in public circulation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the production and placing in circulation of financial instruments if they do not comply with the provisions of the statutes, prospectus or other document issued for this purpose, or for the issue of a deposit certificate without acceptance of the respective deposit —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 18.12.2003., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

194.1 pants. Dissemination of False Data or Information Regarding the State of the Financial System of the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For the dissemination of knowingly false data or information orally, in writing or in any other manner regarding the state of the financial system of the Republic of Latvia —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same actions, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have been committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(13.12.2007. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

195.pants. Laundering of Criminally Obtained Proceeds

(1)	For the laundering of criminally obtained financial proceeds or other property —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of

property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(28.04.2005. version of the law with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

195.1 pants. Failure to Provide Information and Provision of False Information Regarding Ownership of Proceeds and Beneficial Owner

(1)	For knowingly providing false information to a natural or legal person authorised by law to request information about a transaction and the true owner or beneficial owner of the financial proceeds or other property involved therein, as well as for failure to provide information required by law regarding the beneficial owner or knowingly providing false information to a state institution or legal person —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(13.12.2012. version of the law with amendments made by the laws of 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

195.	2 pants. Evasion of Cash Declaration

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For failure to declare or false declaration of a large amount of cash brought into the Republic of Latvia or taken out of it when crossing the internal border of the state, if the completion of a cash declaration in the manner prescribed by law has been requested by an official of the competent authority, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(21) For failure to declare or false declaration of a large amount of cash brought into the Republic of Latvia or taken out of it when crossing the external border of the state —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraphs two and 2.1 of this Section, if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(08.12.2005. version of the law with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017., 20.06.2019., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022. The amendment in paragraph 2.1 replacing the word "piespiedu" with the word "sabiedrisko" enters into force on 01.01.2022. See transitional provision point 26)

196. pants. Abuse and Excess of Authority

(1)	For intentional acts committed by a responsible employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation, that is, a person who has the right in the enterprise (commercial company) or organisation to make decisions binding on other persons, or the right to dispose of the property or financial proceeds of the enterprise (commercial company) or organisation, as well as by such a person authorised by the enterprise (commercial company) or organisation, by abusing their authority or exceeding it, if these acts have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 11.12.2003., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which
enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

197. pants. Negligence

For negligent performance of work duties committed by a responsible employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation or such a person authorised by the enterprise (commercial company) or organisation, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

198. pants. Unlawful Acceptance of Benefits

(1)	For the unlawful acceptance of material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, or an offer thereof, committed personally or through an intermediary by an employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation or a person authorised on the basis of law or a legal transaction to conduct the affairs of another person, for performing or not performing any action using their authority, regardless of whether the accepted material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have been committed on a large scale or have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or material values, a benefit of a proprietary or other nature has been demanded,—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the unlawful acceptance of material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, or an offer thereof, committed personally or through an intermediary by a responsible employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation or a person authorised by an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation with the same status, or a person authorised on the basis of law or a legal transaction to resolve disputes or adopt binding decisions, but who is not a State official, for performing or not performing any action using their authority, regardless of whether the accepted material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to three years.

(4)	For the acts provided for in paragraph three of this Section, if they have been committed on a large scale or have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or material values, a benefit of a proprietary or other nature has been demanded, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of two to five years.
(16.02.2006. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

199. pants. Commercial Bribery

(1)	For offering or giving material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature personally or through an intermediary to an employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation or a person authorised on the basis of law or a legal transaction to conduct the affairs of another person, to a responsible employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation or a person authorised by an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation with the same status, or a person authorised on the basis of law or a legal transaction to resolve disputes, so that they, using their authority, perform or do not perform any action, regardless of whether the material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine and with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to three years.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. un 17.12.2020.
likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

199.	1 pants. Release of the Giver of Benefits from Criminal Liability

A person who has unlawfully offered or given material values, a benefit of a proprietary or other nature may be released from criminal liability if, after committing the criminal acts, they voluntarily report what has occurred and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.
(19.11.2009. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013.)

200. pants

Disclosure of Non-Disclosable Information That Is Not Confidential, Secret or Top Secret Information, Unlawful Acquisition and Disclosure of Information Containing Trade Secrets and Unlawful Disclosure of Financial Market Inside Information

(1)	For the disclosure of non-disclosable information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, if committed by a person who is not a State official and who is responsible for the safekeeping of the information in accordance with law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the unlawful acquisition of a trade secret for one's own or another person's use or disclosure or for the unlawful disclosure of a trade secret to another person for the same purpose, as well as for the unlawful disclosure of financial market inside information —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the theft of the information referred to in paragraph one or two of this Section —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(26.05.2005. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 28.01.2016., 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. un 20.04.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.05.2023.)

201. pants. Usury

For various types of loans that have been made knowingly exploiting the borrower's difficult financial situation and the terms of which are excessively burdensome for them (usury), —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

202. pants. Failure to Ensure the Quality of Goods and Services

For the deliberate manufacture and sale of goods or provision of a service to a consumer that does not meet

the quality requirements established in regulatory enactments, normative technical documents or contracts, as a result of which substantial harm has been caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas
stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

203. pants. Failure to Observe Safety Requirements for Goods and Services

For failure to observe the safety requirements for goods or services established in regulatory enactments, normative technical documents, contracts or regulations approved by appropriately authorised State institutions, as a result of which substantial harm has been caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020.  un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas
stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

204. pants. Defrauding Buyers and Customers

(Izslēgts ar 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013.)

205. Section. Violation of Trading Regulations

(1)	(Deleted by law of 13.12.2012.)
(2)	For deliberate violation of trading regulations issued by state institutions, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

206. Section

Unlawful Use of Trademarks, Other Distinctive Signs and Design Samples

(1)	For unlawful use of a trademark, other distinctive sign of goods or services or a design sample, counterfeiting of a sign or deliberate use or distribution of a counterfeit sign, if committed on a considerable scale or if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For unlawful use of a trademark, other distinctive sign of goods or services or a design sample, counterfeiting of a sign or deliberate use or distribution of a counterfeit sign, if committed on a large scale, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(As worded by law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017.,
17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

207. Section. Entrepreneurial Activity without Registration and without Permit (Licence)

(1)	(Deleted by law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For entrepreneurial activity without registration or without a special permit (licence), if the necessity thereof is prescribed by law, or for continuation of the activity of an enterprise (business company) after an order to suspend its activity, if such entrepreneurial activity or continuation of activity is committed on a considerable scale or if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term from two to five years.
(With amendments made by laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which
enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

208. Section. Prohibited Entrepreneurial Activity

For engaging in entrepreneurial activity in respect of which there is a special prohibition, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term from two to five years.
(With amendments made by laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

209. Section. Fictitious Entrepreneurial Activity

(Deleted by law of 21.05.2009., which enters into force on 01.07.2009.)

210. Section. Dishonest Receipt and Use of Credit and Other Loans

(1)	For providing deliberately false information for the receipt of subsidies, grants, credit or other loans or during the use of subsidies, grants, credit or other loans, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed with the intent to receive or use subsidies, grants, credit or other loans on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by laws of 08.06.2017., 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

211. Section. Unfair Competition, Misleading Advertising and Unfair Commercial Practices

For unfair competition, misleading advertising or unfair commercial practices, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

212. Section

Failure to Comply with Requirements of the State Institution for Competition Protection and Promotion

For failure to comply with lawful requirements of the state institution for competition protection and promotion, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

212.	1 Section. Manipulation of Sports Competitions

(1)	For manipulation of sports competitions organised by a sports organisation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are related to the acceptance, transfer or offer of material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by law of 28.01.2016. with amendments made by law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

213. Section. Bringing to Insolvency

(1)	For bringing a legal person subject to insolvency proceedings to insolvency through negligence, if it has caused substantial harm to the interests of another person protected by law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For intentional bringing of a legal person subject to insolvency proceedings to insolvency, if it has caused substantial harm to the interests of another person protected by law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term from two to five years.
(As worded by law of 13.12.2007. with amendments made by laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

214. pants. Submission of a False Insolvency Petition

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010)

(2)	For the submission of an insolvency petition in which knowingly false information has been provided or information has been concealed, if the petition may result in or has resulted in the declaration of insolvency proceedings (a knowingly false insolvency petition), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by the law of 13.12.2007 with amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010, 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

215. pants. Obstruction of Insolvency Proceedings

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010)

(2)	For providing knowingly false information to the court, a creditors' meeting or other institutions or persons provided for by law, as well as for carrying out transactions in favour of one or more creditors at the expense of other creditors, if committed by an administrator in insolvency proceedings, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For placing obstacles in the course of insolvency proceedings, which is manifested in the conduct of the representative of the debtor (the subject of the insolvency proceedings of a legal person) or of a natural person (in the insolvency proceedings of a natural person) as providing knowingly false information to the court or the administrator, unlawful carrying out of transactions, concealment of property or transactions, concealment, destruction or falsification of documents, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of two to five years.
(As worded by the law of 13.12.2007 with amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010, 13.12.2012, 08.06.2017 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

215.	1 pants. Violation of Legal Protection Proceedings Rules

(1)	For the use of legal protection proceedings for the purpose of evading the performance of obligations — shall be punished by probation supervision or by community service or by a fine.
(2)	For placing obstacles in legal protection proceedings, which is manifested as providing knowingly false information to the court or the person supervising the legal protection proceedings, unlawful carrying out of transactions, concealment of property or transactions, concealment, destruction or falsification of documents, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of two to five years.
(As worded by the law of 13.12.2007 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012, 08.06.2017 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

216. pants. Unlawful Alienation, Damage and Destruction of a Pledged Item

For the alienation of an item pledged in the form of a commercial pledge without the permission of the pledgee or for its damage or destruction, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by the law of 13.12.2012 with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020 and 07.04.2022, which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

217. pants. Violation of Accounting and Statistical Information Rules

(1)	For the concealment or falsification of accounting documents, annual reports, statistical reports or statistical information prescribed by law for an enterprise (business company), institution or organisation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has been committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As worded by the law of 13.12.2012 with amendments made by the laws of 06.06.2019, 17.12.2020 and 07.04.2022, which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

217.	1 pants. Violation of Remuneration Rules
For the payment of remuneration not recorded in accounting records, if it has been committed on a considerable scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community
service, or by a fine and with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of up to three years.
(As worded by the law of 10.03.2016 with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

218. Section. Evasion of Payment of Taxes and Equivalent Payments

(1)	(Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For evasion of payment of taxes or equivalent payments or for concealment or reduction of income, profit or other taxable objects, if it has caused losses to the state or local government in a large amount,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of two to five years.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of two to five years, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020.
which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

218. 1 Section. Indication of a Transaction That Did Not Actually Occur in a Value Added Tax Declaration

(1)	For indication of a transaction subject to value added tax that did not actually occur in a value added tax declaration, if the total value of the transaction or several such transactions that did not actually occur indicated in the declaration reaches a large amount, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For indication of a transaction subject to value added tax that did not actually occur in a value added tax declaration or in several such declarations, if it has caused losses to the state in a substantial amount, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of two to five years.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if they have caused losses to the state in a large amount, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of two to five years, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the Law of 06.07.2021., which comes into force on 05.08.2021. See point 27 of the transitional provisions)

219. Section. Evasion of Submission of a Declaration

(1)	(Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For indication of false information in a declaration of income, property, transactions or other property nature required by law, if false information has been indicated regarding property or other income in a large amount, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For failure to indicate the source of origin of property or other income subject to declaration as required by law or for provision of false information regarding the source of origin of property or other income subject to declaration, if such information has been requested in the manner prescribed by law by a duly authorised state institution and if false information has been indicated regarding property or other income in a large amount, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
