(In the wording of the Law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

220. Section. Absconding of Property

For alienation, damage, destruction, dissipation, concealment or other absconding of property or means of payment with the intent to evade payment of debt or fulfilment of other obligations —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

220. 1 Section. Unlawful Storage, Movement (Transportation) and Sale of Petroleum Products

(1)	For unlawful storage, movement (transportation) or sale of petroleum products, if it has been committed in a substantial amount, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For unlawful storage, movement (transportation) or sale of petroleum products, if it has been committed in a large amount, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the Law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

221. pants

Illegal Manufacturing (Production), Storage, Transportation and Sale of Alcoholic Beverages and Tobacco Products

(1)	For illegal manufacturing (production), storage, transportation or sale of alcoholic beverages or tobacco products, if committed on a considerable scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For illegal manufacturing (production), storage, transportation or sale of alcoholic beverages or tobacco products, if committed on a large scale, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(21.05.2009. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017.,
03.09.2020. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

221.1 pants. Sale of Illegal Alcoholic Beverages

(1)	For the sale of illegally manufactured (produced) or counterfeit alcoholic beverages or spirit-containing liquids which are not alcoholic beverages but which are offered as alcoholic beverages (illegal alcoholic beverages), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed on a considerable scale or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences or if committed on a large scale, or if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(21.06.2007. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 03.09.2020. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

221.2 pants. Illegal Manufacturing (Production), Storage and Transportation of Alcoholic Beverages

(1)	For illegal manufacturing (production), storage or transportation of alcoholic beverages, if committed on a considerable scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For illegal manufacturing (production), storage or transportation of alcoholic beverages, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation

supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(21.06.2007. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 03.09.2020. un
17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

221.3 pants. Acquisition of Illegal Alcoholic Beverages
(Izslēgts ar 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013.)

221.4 pants. Release of a Person from Criminal Liability for Storage and Transportation of Illegal Alcoholic Beverages

A person who has voluntarily surrendered illegal alcoholic beverages and voluntarily reported the manufacturing (production), storage, transportation or sale of these illegal alcoholic beverages shall be released from criminal liability for their storage or transportation.
(21.06.2007. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013.)

221.5 pants. Provision of Premises for Illegal Manufacturing (Production), Storage and Sale of Alcoholic Beverages
(Izslēgts ar 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013.)

221.	6 pants. Illegal Sale of Tobacco Products on a Small Scale

For the sale on a small scale of illegally manufactured (produced) tobacco products or tobacco products which are not marked with excise duty stamps of the Republic of Latvia, except in cases provided for in regulatory enactments, or smokeless tobacco products the placing on the market of which is prohibited, if committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(03.09.2020. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 06.07.2021. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 05.08.2021. Grozījums par vārdu aizstāšanu "vai ar piespiedu darbu" ar vārdiem "vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu" stājas spēkā 01.01.2022. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 26. punktu)

222. pants. Violation of Veterinary Regulations

(1)	For intentional violation of veterinary regulations, if it has caused the spread of an epizootic or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community
service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

223. pants. Violation of Regulations on Combating Plant Diseases and Pests

(1)	For violation of regulations on combating plant diseases or pests, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

XX	nodaļa Criminal Offences against
General Safety and Public Order

224. pants. Banditry

(1)	For the association of more than two persons in an organised, armed group (gang) for the commission of crimes
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For participation in crimes committed by a gang or for leading it —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011. un 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 11.punktu)

225. Section. Mass Disorder

For organising such mass disorder which is connected with riots, destruction, arson, destruction of property or with violence against a person, or with resistance to representatives of authority, or for active participation therein, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 21.10.2010 with amendments made by the law of 08.07.2011, which comes into force on 01.10.2011. See transitional provision paragraph 11)

225.1 Section. Violation of Restrictions and Prohibitions Established During a State of Emergency and State of Exception

(1) For violation of restrictions or prohibitions established during a state of emergency, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For violation of restrictions or prohibitions established during a state of exception, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(3) For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(In the wording of the law of 26.04.2018 with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020 and the law of 07.04.2022, which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

226. Section. Violation of the Procedure for Organising and Holding Public Events

(1) For violation of the procedure for organising or holding public events, committed by the organiser of the event or another person, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(3) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if as a result thereof the death of two or more persons has occurred, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(In the wording of the law of 16.12.2004 with amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012, 25.09.2014, 17.12.2020 and the law of 07.04.2022, which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

227. Section

Endangering Public Safety, Order and Personal Health by Performing Religious Activities

For organising or leading such a group whose activities, manifesting themselves as the preaching of religious teachings and the performance of religious rituals, are connected with causing harm to public safety and order, personal health, legally protected personal interests, or for a person's participation in such activities —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the law of 12.02.2004, 13.12.2012 and the law of 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

228. Section. Desecration of a Grave and Corpse

(1) For desecration of a grave, burial urn, buried or unburied corpse —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they are connected with the theft of a monument, burial urn or other objects located on a grave or in a grave, or at a burial urn, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(With amendments made by the law of 12.02.2004, 13.12.2012 and the law of 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

229. Section. Unlawful Activities with Cultural Objects

(1) For unlawful storage, movement, dispatch, alienation of a cultural object, that is, a cultural monument under the protection of the Republic of Latvia or another state, a museum collection object or a specially protected library collection document, or for its unlawful export from the Republic of Latvia or import into the Republic of Latvia, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For destruction, damage or desecration of a cultural object —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 07.01.2021 with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

229.1 Section. Unlawful Activities with Antiquities

(1) For unlawful acquisition, storage, movement, dispatch, alienation of an antiquity under the protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under the protection of another state, or for its unlawful export from the Republic of Latvia or import into the Republic of Latvia —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For destruction or damage of an antiquity under the protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under the protection of another state —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 07.01.2021 with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

229.2 Section. Release of a Person from Criminal Liability for Unlawful Acquisition, Storage, Movement and Dispatch of Antiquities

A person who has voluntarily surrendered an unlawfully acquired, stored, moved or dispatched antiquity under the protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under the protection of another state, shall be released from criminal liability for unlawful acquisition, storage, movement or dispatch of an antiquity under the protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under the protection of another state.
(In the wording of the law of 07.01.2021, which comes into force on 02.02.2021.)

230. pants. Cruel treatment of an animal and torture of an animal

(1)	For cruel treatment of an animal as a result of which it has perished or been maimed, or for torture of an animal —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to keep animals of a specific or all species for a term not exceeding three years.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed in a public place or in the presence of a minor or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if substantial harm has been caused thereby, or for torture of an animal as a result of which it has perished or been maimed, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to keep animals of a specific or all species for a term not exceeding five years.
(21.05.2009. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 22.06.2017., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

230.1 pants. Violation of animal keeping regulations

(1)	For violation of animal keeping regulations, if as a result thereof medium serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For violation of animal keeping regulations, if as a result thereof serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim or it has caused the death of a person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(25.04.2002. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 11.06.2020. un 17.12.2020.

likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

231.pants. Hooliganism

(1)	For gross disturbance of public order which manifests itself in obvious disrespect for society or in shamelessness, ignoring generally accepted norms of conduct and disturbing the peace of persons, the work of institutions or enterprises (commercial companies), or organisations (hooliganism), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For hooliganism, if it has been committed by a group of persons or if it is connected with causing bodily injury to the victim or with damaging or destroying property, or with resisting a representative of authority or a person who opposes the violation of public order, or if it has been committed using weapons, as well as other objects usable for causing bodily injury, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 25.09.2014. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas
stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

231.	1 pants. Knowingly false notification of the placement or positioning of an explosive, poisonous, radioactive or bacteriological substance or material or explosive device

(1)	For a knowingly false notification of the placement of an explosive, poisonous, radioactive or bacteriological substance or material or explosive device in an institution, enterprise or other object or positioning outside an institution, enterprise or other object —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if substantial harm has been caused thereby or if the knowingly false notification is directed against two or more objects, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by a fine.
(25.04.2002. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 10.03.2016., 11.06.2020.,
17.12.2020. un 14.12.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 30.12.2023.)

232. pants. Involvement of mentally ill persons in a criminal offence

For involvement of a person in a criminal offence, knowing that he or she suffers from mental disorders, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

233. pants

Unauthorised manufacture, repair, acquisition, storage, carrying, transportation, forwarding, sale and violation of sale regulations of firearms, essential components of firearms, ammunition for firearms, high-energy pneumatic weapons, explosives and explosive devices

(1)	For sale of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, ammunition for a firearm, a high-energy pneumatic

weapon, explosives or an explosive device to a person who does not have the relevant permit or special permit (licence), if it has been committed by a person who has the relevant sale permit or special permit (licence), —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For manufacture, repair, acquisition, storage, carrying, transportation, forwarding or sale of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, ammunition for a firearm, a high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosives or an explosive device without the relevant permit —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if they have been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding ten years, with or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term not exceeding three years.
(20.05.2004. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

234. pants

Unauthorised manufacture and use of gas pistols (revolvers) and their ammunition

(1)	For manufacture of a gas pistol (revolver) or ammunition intended for it filled with substances of irritating or paralysing effect without the relevant permit —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For violation of the conditions or procedure for use or utilisation of a gas pistol (revolver), if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For violation of the conditions or procedure for use or utilisation of a gas pistol (revolver), if serious consequences have been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 29.10.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

235. pants

Release of a person from criminal liability for the manufacture, acquisition, carrying and storage of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosive or explosive device and for the manufacture of a gas pistol (revolver) and its ammunition

A person who has voluntarily surrendered a firearm, essential component of a firearm, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosive or explosive device manufactured, acquired, carried or stored without the relevant permit or a gas pistol (revolver) or its ammunition manufactured without the relevant permit shall be released from criminal liability if the person's actions do not contain the elements of another criminal offence.
(In the wording of the law of 20.05.2004 with amendments made by the law of 29.10.2015, which comes into force on 03.12.2015.)

236. pants

Storage, carrying, transportation and forwarding of firearms, essential components of firearms, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapons, explosives and explosive devices in violation of regulatory enactments

(1)	For the storage, carrying, transportation or forwarding of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosive or explosive device in violation of regulatory enactments, if as a result thereof any of the said objects has been lost or acquired by another person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same offence, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 20.05.2004 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012, 29.10.2015 and 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

237. pants

Violation of the conditions or procedure for the use or application of firearms and high-energy pneumatic weapons and violation of the procedure for the use of explosives and explosive devices

(1)	For violation of the conditions or procedure for the use or application of a firearm or high-energy pneumatic weapon or violation of the procedure for the use of explosives and explosive devices, if committed by a person who is permitted to acquire, store or carry a firearm or high-energy pneumatic weapon or who has the right to use explosives and explosive devices, and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012 with amendments made by the laws of 29.10.2015 and 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

237.	1 pants. Violation of the rules for the circulation of goods of strategic importance

(1)	For violation of the rules for the circulation of goods of strategic importance, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For violation of the prohibition on the circulation of equipment, devices or instruments and their components or software specially created or adapted for operational activities to be carried out in a special manner or for obstructing them —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term not exceeding five years.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012 with amendments made by the laws of 12.11.2015, 26.04.2018 and 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

238. pants. Violation of production safety and technical discipline rules

(1)	For violation of production safety or technical discipline rules in construction works or works related to blasting or in enterprises (companies) exposed to explosion hazards, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term not exceeding five years.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with production safety or technical discipline rules, or for the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding eight years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term not exceeding ten years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012, 25.09.2014, 17.12.2020, 07.01.2021 and 17.12.2020, which
comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

239. pants. Violation of construction rules

(1)	For the performance of construction works at a time when they have been suspended, in a third group structure or in a multi-dwelling building, if the construction works have been suspended due to their performance without a building permit or due to the commencement of construction works before the fulfilment of the conditions of the building permit, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For violation of construction norms or rules for buildings, bridges, overpasses or other structures, if as a result thereof a structure or part thereof has collapsed, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term not exceeding five years.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term not exceeding seven years.
(4)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding eight years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or
the right to hold a specific position for a term not exceeding ten years.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014 with amendments made by the laws of 07.01.2021 and 17.12.2020, which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

239.	1 pants. Operation of a dangerous structure

(1)	For violation of the prohibition on the operation of a dangerous structure, if committed by a person who is responsible for the operation of the structure, and if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding eight years.
(In the wording of the law of 11.06.2020 with amendments made by the law of 06.07.2021, which comes into force on 05.08.2021. The amendment in paragraph one replacing the words "or by forced labour" with the words "or by probation supervision, or by community service" comes into force on 01.01.2022. See transitional provision 26)

240. Section. Violation of Fire Safety Regulations

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012)

(2)	For intentional violation of fire safety regulations, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if it has caused substantial harm —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For intentional violation of fire safety regulations, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(4)	For intentional violation of fire safety regulations, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if it has caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012, 25.09.2014 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

241. Section. Unauthorised Access to an Automated Data Processing System

(1)	For unauthorised access to the resources of an automated data processing system, if it is connected with overcoming the system's protection means or if it is committed without appropriate authorisation or by using rights granted to another person and if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if it is committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if they are committed by an organised group or if they have caused serious consequences, or if they are directed against an automated data processing system that processes information related to the political, economic, military, social or other security of the state, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014 with amendments made by the laws of 06.07.2021 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

242. Section. Unauthorised Acquisition of Computer Software

(Deleted by the law of 28.04.2005, which enters into force on 01.06.2005.)

243. Section

Disruption of the Operation of an Automated Data Processing System and Unlawful Actions with Information Included in this System

(1)	For unauthorised alteration, damage, destruction, deterioration or concealment of information contained in an automated data processing system or for the input of deliberately false information into an automated data processing system, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For deliberate disruption of the operation of an automated data processing system by inputting, transferring, damaging, deleting, deteriorating, altering or concealing information, if it damages or destroys the protection system and causes substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if it is committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation

supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012)

(5)	For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences or if they are directed against an automated data processing system that processes information related to the political, economic, military, social or other security of the state, or for the criminal offence provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if it is committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007, 08.07.2011, 13.12.2012, 25.09.2014,
08.06.2017, 17.12.2020 and 27.10.2022, which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)

244. Section

Unlawful Actions with Devices for Influencing the Resources of an Automated Data Processing System

(1)	For unauthorised manufacture, adaptation for use, sale, distribution, acquisition, transfer or storage of a tool (device, computer program, computer password, access code or similar data) which is intended for influencing the resources of an automated data processing system or with the help of which it is possible to access an automated data processing system or a part thereof for the purpose of committing a criminal offence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they are committed by an organised group or if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012, 25.09.2014, 06.07.2021 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

244. 1 Section. Acquisition, Manufacture, Alteration, Storage and Distribution of Data, Software and Equipment for Unlawful Actions with Electronic Communications Network Terminal Equipment

For the alteration of data necessary for the identification of electronic communications network terminal equipment in an electronic communications network or for the acquisition, storage or distribution of data intended for such purpose, as well as for the acquisition, manufacture, storage or distribution of software or equipment intended for such purpose without the consent of the manufacturer or a person authorised by it, if such acts are committed with mercenary intent or if they are committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, or if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

245. Section. Violation of Information System Security Regulations

For violation of information storage and processing regulations developed in accordance with the information regime or its protection or other information computer system security regulations, committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, if it has been the reason for the theft, destruction or damage of information or if it has caused other substantial harm, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

246. pants

Unauthorised Dispatch of Highly Flammable Substances and Objects, as well as Corrosive Substances

(1)	For unauthorised dispatch of highly flammable substances or objects, as well as corrosive substances, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

247. pants. Unauthorised Transport of Highly Flammable Substances and Objects on an Aircraft

(1)	For unauthorised transport of highly flammable substances or objects on an aircraft —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

248. pants

Unauthorised Manufacture, Acquisition, Storage, Sale and Dispatch of Poisonous and Potent Substances

(1)	For the manufacture, acquisition, storage or sale of poisonous or potent substances, which are not narcotic or psychotropic substances, without the relevant authorisation, as well as for violation of the regulations regarding the manufacture, storage, dispensing, accounting, transport or dispatch of such substances, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For unauthorised manufacture, acquisition or sale of the substances provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(4)	For unauthorised manufacture, acquisition or sale of the substances provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

248.1 pants. Unauthorised Manufacture, Acquisition, Storage, Transport and Dispatch for the Purpose of Sale and Sale of New Psychoactive Substances

(1)	For the manufacture, acquisition, storage, transport or dispatch for the purpose of sale or sale of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by probation supervision, or by community service.
(In the wording of the law of 03.04.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 15.05.2014., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

248.	2 pants. Unauthorised Manufacture, Acquisition, Storage, Transport, Dispatch of New Psychoactive Substances and Unauthorised Use of New Psychoactive Substances

(1)	For unauthorised acquisition, storage, transport or dispatch of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, without the intention to sell them or unauthorised use of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, if it has been committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for unauthorised acquisition, storage, transport, dispatch and use of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For unauthorised manufacture of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, without the intention to sell them —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

249. pants

Violation of Regulations on the Production, Acquisition, Storage, Accounting, Dispensing, Transport and Dispatch of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances

(1)	For violation of the regulations on the production, acquisition, storage, accounting, dispensing, transport or dispatch of narcotic or psychotropic substances, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

250. Section. Unlawful Dispensing of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances

(1)	For prescribing a prescription without medical necessity or unlawfully issuing another document for receiving a narcotic or psychotropic substance, as well as for dispensing a narcotic or psychotropic substance without a prescription or other document or knowing that the prescription or other document is fictitious or unlawfully issued, if such acts are committed with mercenary intent or due to other personal interest, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years.

(2)	For prescribing a prescription without medical necessity or unlawfully issuing another document for receiving a narcotic or psychotropic substance, as well as for dispensing a narcotic or psychotropic substance without a prescription or other document or knowing that the prescription or other document is fictitious or unlawfully issued, if it is committed with narcotic or psychotropic substances in large amounts or has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in the first or second part of this Section, if it is committed by an organised group or if it has caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 07.01.2021. and 17.12.2020., which
enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

251. Section

Incitement to Use Narcotic, Psychotropic and New Psychoactive Substances

(1)	For inciting the use of narcotic or psychotropic substances or new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, or for providing premises for the use of these substances —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they are committed against a minor, a mentally ill person or a person who is subject to narcological treatment or who was in material or other dependence on the guilty person, or if substances that enhance their effect are added to the narcotic or psychotropic substances, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For inciting the use of narcotic or psychotropic substances or new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, if the use of these substances has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

252. Section

Introduction of Narcotic, Psychotropic and New Psychoactive Substances Against a Person's Will

(1)	For introducing narcotic or psychotropic substances or new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, to another person or adding them to a product intended for use by another person against that person's will or without that person's knowledge —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(2)	For the same acts, if substances that enhance their effect are added to the narcotic or psychotropic substances, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For the acts provided for in the first or second part of this Section, if they are committed against a minor or using violence, or threatening to use violence or if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

253. pants

Unlawful manufacture, acquisition, possession, transportation and forwarding of narcotic and psychotropic substances

(1)	For unlawful manufacture, acquisition, possession, transportation or forwarding of narcotic or psychotropic substances without the intention to distribute them —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if they have been committed with narcotic or psychotropic substances in large quantities, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

253.1 pants. Unlawful manufacture, acquisition, possession, transportation and forwarding of narcotic and psychotropic substances for the purpose of distribution and unlawful distribution

(1)	For unlawful manufacture, acquisition, possession, transportation or forwarding of narcotic or psychotropic substances for the purpose of distribution or for unlawful distribution of narcotic or psychotropic substances —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by an organised group or if they have been committed with narcotic or psychotropic substances in large quantities, or if they have caused serious consequences, as well as for distribution of narcotic or psychotropic substances to a minor, in educational institutions or on their premises, in restaurants, cafés, bars, public entertainment or festive event venues —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 07.01.2021., which come into force on 02.02.2021.)

253.	2 pants. Unlawful acquisition, possession and distribution of narcotic and psychotropic substances in small quantities and unlawful use of narcotic and psychotropic substances

(1)	For unlawful acquisition or possession of narcotic or psychotropic substances in small quantities without the intention to distribute them or unlawful use of narcotic or psychotropic substances, if committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for unlawful acquisition, possession and use of narcotic and psychotropic substances, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For unlawful manufacture, acquisition, possession, transportation or forwarding of narcotic or psychotropic substances in small quantities for the purpose of distribution or for unlawful distribution of narcotic or psychotropic substances in small quantities —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the Law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

254. pants

Release of a person from criminal liability for acquisition, possession, transportation and forwarding of narcotic and psychotropic substances

A person who has voluntarily surrendered narcotic, psychotropic, new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, or has voluntarily reported their acquisition, possession, transportation or forwarding, shall be released from criminal liability for the use, acquisition, possession, transportation or forwarding of these substances.
(In the wording of the Law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the Law of 25.09.2014., which comes into force on 29.10.2014.)

255. pants

Manufacture, acquisition, possession, transportation, forwarding and distribution of equipment and substances (precursors) intended for unlawful manufacture of narcotic and psychotropic substances

(1)	For manufacture, acquisition, possession, transportation or forwarding of equipment, devices, objects, materials or substances (precursors exceeding a small quantity) intended for unlawful manufacture of narcotic or psychotropic substances
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed with the intention to distribute such equipment, devices, objects, materials or substances (precursors), or for distribution of equipment, devices, objects, materials or substances (precursors) intended for unlawful manufacture of narcotic or psychotropic substances —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 25.04.2002., 21.06.2007., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

256. pants

Unlawful sowing and cultivation of plants containing narcotic or psychotropic substances

(1)	For unlawful sowing or cultivation of plants containing narcotic or psychotropic substances —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For unlawful sowing or cultivation of plants containing narcotic or psychotropic substances in large areas, or if committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 07.01.2021. and 17.12.2020., which
come into force on 01.01.2022.)

XXI	nodaļa
Criminal offences against traffic safety

257. pants

Violation of safety and operation rules for railway, water and air transport traffic

(1)	For violation of safety or operation rules for railway, water or air transport traffic or for deliberate putting into operation of technically defective railway, water or air transport, committed by a transport

employee, if it has substantially disrupted transport operations, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(2)	For the same offence, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

258. Section. Damage to Traffic Routes and Railway, Water and Air Transport Vehicles

(1)	For intentional destruction, damage or other intentional actions of traffic routes, road structures or devices, railway, water or air transport vehicles, transport telecommunication networks or signalling means, electronic or communication equipment, as a result of which they have become unfit for operation, as well as if the said actions have caused disruptions in transport operations, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

259. Section. Arbitrary Stopping of a Train

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For arbitrary stopping of a train without necessity by means of an emergency brake, by disconnecting the brake air line or otherwise, if it has caused a catastrophe, damage to rolling stock or other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(3)	For the same actions, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

260. Section

Violation of Traffic Rules and Vehicle Operation Rules

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 11.06.2020.)

(11) For violation of traffic rules or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle, and if as a result thereof medium serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For violation of traffic rules or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle, and if as a result thereof serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim or it has caused the death of a person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to drive a vehicle for a term of up to five years.

(3)	For violation of traffic rules or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle, and if it has caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with deprivation of the right to drive a vehicle for a term of up to seven years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 12.02.2004., 06.10.2005., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014.,
29.10.2015., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

261. Section. Concept of Vehicle

Vehicles referred to in Sections 175, 176, 260, 262-265 and 284 of this Law shall mean all types of automobiles, tractors and other self-propelled machines, trams, trolleybuses, motorcycles and other mechanical vehicles that move with their own energy source, except for vehicles with an internal combustion engine whose working volume is less than 50 cubic centimetres or an electric motor whose maximum power does not exceed four kilowatts and the maximum speed provided for in the design does not exceed 45 kilometres per hour.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 27.10.2022., which come into force on 25.11.2022.)

262. pants

Driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances

(1)	For driving a vehicle, if there is no driving licence for the appropriate category of vehicle (a driving licence has not been obtained in the prescribed manner or has been withdrawn) and if the driver is under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the driving licence for five years.

(11) For driving a vehicle or teaching to drive a vehicle, if the alcohol concentration in the blood established by examination of exhaled air or blood exceeds 1.5 per mille, or for driving a vehicle or teaching to drive a vehicle while under the influence of narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the driving licence for five years.

(2)	For violation of traffic rules or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if as a result thereof slight bodily injury has been caused to the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the driving licence for five years.

(3)	For violation of traffic rules or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if as a result thereof bodily injury of medium gravity has been caused to the victim, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the driving licence for a term of five to seven years.

(4)	For violation of traffic rules or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if as a result thereof serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim or it has caused the death of a person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to ten years, with withdrawal of the driving licence for a term of five to ten years.

(5)	For violation of traffic rules or vehicle operation rules, if committed by

a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if it has caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years, with withdrawal of the driving licence for a term of five to ten years.
(29.10.2015. version of the law with amendments made by the laws of 22.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)

262. 1 pants. Refusal to undergo examination for the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic and other intoxicating substances and leaving the scene of a traffic accident

(1)	For refusal to undergo medical examination to determine alcohol concentration in the blood or examination for the influence of narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, if committed by a vehicle driver, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the driving licence for five years.

(2)	For refusal to undergo medical examination to determine alcohol concentration in the blood or examination for the influence of narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, or for leaving the scene of a traffic accident after a traffic accident in violation of the prescribed procedure, if committed by a vehicle driver who caused a traffic accident as a result of which slight, medium or serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim or as a result of which the death of a person has occurred, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the driving licence for a term of five to seven years.
(29.10.2015. version of the law with amendments made by the laws of 11.06.2020., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)

263. pants. Putting technically defective vehicles into operation

For deliberate putting of technically defective vehicles into operation or violation of other operation rules guaranteeing traffic safety, committed by a person who is responsible for the technical condition or operation of vehicles, if as a result thereof bodily injury of medium gravity or serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim or it has caused the death of a person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the right to engage in a particular occupation for a term of up to five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which
enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

264. pants

Allowing persons who are under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances to drive vehicles

For allowing a person who is under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances to drive a vehicle, if as a result thereof slight, medium gravity or serious bodily injury has been caused to the victim or it has caused the death of a person, a person who is responsible for the technical condition or operation of vehicles, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with withdrawal of the right to engage in a particular occupation for a term of up to five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

265. pants

Unlawful manufacture, sale, issuance, forgery, destruction, theft of a vehicle registration document, unit and registration number plate and use of a forgery

(1)	For unlawful manufacture, sale, issuance, forgery, destruction of a vehicle registration document, unit or registration number plate, or use of a forged registration number plate
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For theft of a vehicle registration document or registration number plate —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in the first or second part of this Article, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)
