266. Section. Violation of Transport Traffic Rules

(1)	For violation of transport traffic order or safety protection rules, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

267. Section. Failure to Report Vessel Name in Case of Vessel Collision

For failure to report to another vessel the name of one's vessel and its port of registration, as well as the place of departure or destination, after collision with that vessel, although it was possible to provide this information, the captain —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

268. Section. Seizure of Air and Water Transport Vehicle

(1)	For seizure of an air or water transport vehicle, excluding small-sized transport vehicles, on the ground, in waters or in flight —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if they have been committed using violence or threatening with violence, or if they have caused an accident or other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to twelve years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(3)	For the acts provided for in the first or second part of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to fifteen years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three

years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 25.09.2014., which enters into force on 29.10.2014. See transitional provision point 16)

XXII	Chapter
Criminal Offences Against the Order of Administration

269. Section. Attack on Representative of Authority and Other State Official

(1)	For an attack on a representative of authority or other state official in connection with their lawful official activity, as well as for an attack on a person in connection with their participation in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if as a result of the attack serious bodily injury has been caused or other serious consequences have been caused or if the attack has been committed by an organised group, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which
enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

270. Section. Resistance to Representative of Authority and Other State Official

(1)	For resistance to a representative of authority or other state official, if they are performing their assigned official duties, or for resistance to a person, if they are participating in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or for coercing these persons to perform manifestly unlawful acts, if the resistance or coercion has been committed using violence or threatening with violence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

271. Section. Insult to Honour and Dignity of Representative of Authority and Other State Official

(Deleted by the law of 22.01.2004., which enters into force on 01.02.2004.)

272. Section. Provision of False Information to State Institution

For knowingly providing false information to a state institution, including to a parliamentary inquiry commission, if committed by a person who, in accordance with law, has a duty to provide information to a state institution or parliamentary inquiry commission, or for refusal to give an explanation, opinion or translation to a parliamentary inquiry commission —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

272.	1 Section. Coercion to Give False Explanation, Opinion or Translation to Parliamentary Inquiry Commission

(1)	For bribery of a person or other unlawful influencing with the purpose of causing a person to give a false explanation, opinion or translation or to refuse to give an explanation, opinion or translation to a parliamentary inquiry commission, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they are connected with violence or with threat of violence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(3)	For the acts provided for in the first part of this Section, if they are connected with torture, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
(In the wording of the law of 18.12.2003. with amendments made by the laws of 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

273. Section. Arbitrary Assumption of Title and Authority of State Official

For arbitrary assumption of the title or authority of a state official with the purpose of committing a criminal offence — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

274. Section. Theft and Destruction of Document, Seal and Stamp

(1)	For theft, concealment, intentional destruction or damage of a document that grants rights or releases from obligations, a seal or stamp, as well as for use or realisation of a stolen document, seal or stamp —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

275. pants

Forgery of a Document, Seal and Stamp and Realisation and Use of a Forged Document, Seal and Stamp

(1)	For forgery of a document that grants rights or releases from obligations, a seal or a stamp, as well as for realisation or use of a forged document, seal or stamp —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed for mercenary purposes or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if they have caused substantial harm, —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding four years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which
come into force on 04.05.2022.)

275.1 pants. Obtaining an Identity Document Using Another Person's Data

For obtaining an identity document using another person's data, —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

275.2 pants. Obtaining and Possession of a Forged Interoperable Certificate

For obtaining or possessing a forged interoperable vaccination, testing or recovery certificate —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 11.11.2021. with amendments made by the law of 07.04.2022., which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

275.	3 pants. Release from Criminal Liability of the Obtainer, Possessor and User of a Forged Interoperable Certificate

A person who has obtained, possessed or used a forged interoperable vaccination, testing or recovery certificate may be released from criminal liability if, after committing the criminal acts, he or she voluntarily reports what has occurred and actively facilitates the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.
(In the wording of the law of 11.11.2021., which comes into force on 14.11.2021.)

276. pants. Unlawful Opening and Destruction of a Postal Item

(1)	For unlawful opening or destruction of a postal item —

the applicable sentence is short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed by an employee of postal, railway, water or air transport, —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

277. pants. Unauthorised Actions with Archival Documents

For damaging, concealing or unlawfully destroying, alienating, storing, moving, transmitting or exporting outside the Republic of Latvia or importing into the Republic of Latvia an archival document under the protection of the Republic of Latvia or another state, a document with archival value or a copy replacing such documents, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 07.01.2021., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

278. pants. Forgery of Postal Payment Stamps

For forgery of postage stamps or other postal payment stamps or international postal reply coupons, as well as for use of forged or used postage stamps or other postal payment stamps or international postal reply coupons for postal items or sale for the same purpose —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

279. pants. Arbitrary Conduct

(1)	For performing an action arbitrarily, circumventing the procedure established in a regulatory enactment, if the lawfulness of this action is disputed by a state or local government institution or another person (arbitrary conduct) and if substantial harm has been caused as a result of this action, —

the applicable sentence is short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(3)	For arbitrary conduct, if it is associated with violence or threat of violence or if serious consequences have been caused thereby, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation
supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

280. pants. Violation of the Provisions for Employment of a Person

(1)	For violation of the restrictions or provisions for employment of a person provided for by law, if it has been committed by an employer and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

the applicable sentence is short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For employment of a person who is not entitled to stay in the Republic of Latvia, if it has been committed by an employer and if a minor has been employed or if more than five persons have been employed, or if a person has been employed in particularly exploitative working conditions, or if a victim of trafficking in human beings has been knowingly employed, —

the applicable sentence is short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 16.06.2011. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

281. pants. Concealment of a Person's Identity

(1)	For concealment of one's own identity, staying in the Republic of Latvia without a proper identity document or using another person's or forged identity document, —

the applicable sentence is short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For concealment of one's own identity, if it has been committed with the intent to evade criminal liability or administrative liability or to commit a criminal offence or with the intent to help another person evade criminal liability or administrative liability, —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

281.1 pants. Impersonation of Another Person

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For impersonation of another person when taking a Latvian language proficiency test or other knowledge test provided for in the Citizenship Law, in order to create an opportunity for that person to acquire Latvian citizenship through naturalisation, if it has been committed for mercenary purposes, —

the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 21.06.2007. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

282.pants. Evasion of conscription for compulsory military service

(Deleted by the law of 14.12.2006, which enters into force on 01.01.2007.)

282.1 pants. Evasion of mobilisation

(1)	For intentional evasion of mobilisation, if committed by a person who is subject to mobilisation for the implementation of civil protection measures, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a reserve soldier, reservist or National Guard member, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(As worded by the law of 17.10.2002 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)

282.2 pants. Obstruction of mobilisation activities and non-fulfilment of mobilisation request

(1)	For intentional obstruction of the execution of an order of a competent state institution during mobilisation or for intentional obstruction of the execution of a municipal order when implementing civil protection measures, or for intentional non-fulfilment of other mobilisation activities or mobilisation request —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(As worded by the law of 17.10.2002 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

283. pants. Violation of state border regime

(Deleted by the law of 11.12.2003, which enters into force on 02.01.2004.)

284. pants. Unlawful crossing of state border

(1)	For intentional unlawful crossing of the external state border —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(11) For intentional unlawful crossing of the state border, if committed by a person for whom a prohibition on departure from the Republic of Latvia has been imposed to prevent a threat to national security, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one, if committed by a group of persons or using a vehicle, or without observing the imposed prohibition on entry into the Republic of Latvia, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
(As worded by the law of 13.12.2012 with amendments made by the laws of 08.06.2017., 26.04.2018., 17.12.2020. and 14.12.2023., which enters into force on 30.12.2023.)

285. Section. Unlawful Movement of Persons Across the State Border

(1)	For unlawful movement of a person across the state border, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a state official using their official position, or by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or for unlawful movement of several persons across the state border in one instance —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For the same acts, if committed by an organised group or if they have caused serious consequences, or for unlawful movement of a large number of persons, that is, more than five persons in one instance, across the state border —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(31) For the same acts, if committed during the operation of an enhanced border protection system or during a state of emergency declared due to a threat to the inviolability of the state border, regardless of the place of commission of the offence in the territory of Latvia —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(4)	For the same acts, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 11.12.2003., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014.,
10.03.2016., 17.12.2020. and 05.10.2023., which enters into force on 20.10.2023. See transitional provision point 25.2)

285.1 Section. Provision of Opportunity to Reside Unlawfully in the Republic of Latvia

(1)	For deliberate provision of opportunity for a person to reside unlawfully in the Republic of Latvia, if committed by a group of persons or by a state official using their official position, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(2)	For deliberate provision of opportunity for a person to reside unlawfully in the Republic of Latvia, if committed with mercenary intent or if such opportunity has been provided to two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For deliberate provision of opportunity for a person to reside unlawfully in the Republic of Latvia, if committed by an organised group or if it has caused serious consequences, or for provision of opportunity for a large number of persons, that is, more than five persons in one instance, to reside unlawfully in the Republic of Latvia —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 10.03.2016., 17.12.2020. and 05.10.2023., which enters into force on 20.10.2023. See transitional provision point 25.2)

285.	2 Section. Abusive Provision of Opportunity to Lawfully Obtain the Right to Reside in the Republic of Latvia, Another Member State of the European Union, a State of the European Economic Area or the Swiss Confederation

(1)	For abusive provision of opportunity for a person to lawfully obtain the right to reside in the Republic of Latvia, another Member State of the European Union, a State of the European Economic Area or the Swiss Confederation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(2)	For abusive provision of opportunity for a person to lawfully obtain the right to reside in the Republic of Latvia, another Member State of the European Union, a State of the European Economic Area or the Swiss Confederation, if committed with mercenary intent or if such opportunity has been provided to two or more persons, or if committed by a group of persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For abusive provision of opportunity for a person to lawfully obtain the right to reside in the Republic of Latvia, another Member State of the European Union, a State of the European Economic Area or the Swiss Confederation, if committed by an organised group or if it has caused serious consequences, or for provision of opportunity for a large number of persons, that is, more than five persons in one instance, to lawfully obtain the right to reside in the Republic of Latvia, another Member State of the European Union, a State of the European Economic Area or the Swiss Confederation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 05.10.2023., which enters into force on 20.10.2023. See transitional provision point 25.2)

286. Section. Unauthorised Raising of the Flag of the State of Latvia on a Vessel

For raising the flag of the State of Latvia on a vessel, if there is no right to do so, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

287. Section

Unauthorised Use of the Signs of the Red Cross, Red Crescent and Blue and White Shield

For unauthorised use of the signs of the Red Cross, Red Crescent or Blue and White Shield, as well as the name of the Red Cross or Red Crescent —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 18.12.2003. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

288. pants

Damage to Telecommunications Equipment, Radio and Television Transmitters and Postal Technological Equipment

For intentional destruction or damage to telecommunications equipment, a radio or television transmitter or postal technological equipment, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(13.12.2012. law version with amendments made by 17.12.2020. law, which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

288.1 pants. Violation of the Procedure for Installation and Use of Equipment Prescribed in Regulatory Enactments for Broadcasting and Distribution of Radio and Television Programmes

(1)	(Deleted by 13.12.2012. law)

(2)	For violation of the procedure for installation or use of radio wave emitting equipment prescribed in regulatory enactments for broadcasting or distribution of radio or television programmes, as well as cable network systems for distribution of television or sound broadcasting signals, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(31.10.2002. law version with amendments made by 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022. law, which comes into force 04.05.2022.)

288.2 pants. Unlawful Financing of a Political Organisation (Party) or Association of Political Organisations (Parties)

(1)	For unlawful financing of a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) in a large amount —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(08.09.2011. law version with amendments made by 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. law, which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

288.3 pants. Intermediation in Unlawful Financing of a Political Organisation (Party) or Association of Political Organisations (Parties)

For intermediation in unlawful financing of a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) in a large amount —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(08.09.2011. law version with amendments made by 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. law, which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

288.4 pants. Acceptance and Extortion of Unlawful Financing of a Political Organisation (Party) or Association of Political Organisations (Parties)

(1)	For acceptance of unlawful financing of a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) in a large amount —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if they are related to demanding financing of a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) in a large amount, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For extortion of financing of a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(08.09.2011. law version with amendments made by 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. law, which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

288.	5 pants. Release from Criminal Liability of a Person Who Has Unlawfully Financed Political Organisations (Parties) or Associations of Political Organisations (Parties)

(1)	A person who has unlawfully financed a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) may be released from criminal liability if the unlawful financing of the political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) is related to extortion or if this person, after the unlawful financing of the political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties), voluntarily reports what has occurred and actively facilitates the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.

(2)	Extortion shall be understood as demanding financing of a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties), which is related to threats to harm the lawful interests of a person.

(3)	A supporter and intermediary of unlawful financing of a political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties) may be released from criminal liability if he or she, after the unlawful financing of the political organisation (party) or association of political organisations (parties), voluntarily reports what has occurred and actively facilitates the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.
(08.09.2011. law version with amendments made by 13.12.2012. law, which comes into force 01.04.2013.)

XXIII	nodaļa
Criminal Offences against Jurisdiction

289. pants. Falsification of Evidence

(1)	For deliberately creating false evidence or deliberately concealing existing evidence, if committed by a judge, prosecutor or investigator, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or in cases concerning serious or particularly serious crimes, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009.,13.12.2012. and 17.12.2022. law, which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

290. pants. Bringing a Person to Criminal Liability, Knowing That the Person Is Not Guilty

(1)	For bringing a person to criminal liability, knowing that the person is not guilty, if committed by a prosecutor, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they are related to an accusation of committing a serious or particularly serious crime or to falsification of evidence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. law, which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

291. pants. Unlawful Rendering of a Judgment or Decision

(1)	For deliberately rendering an unlawful judgment or decision, if committed by a judge, prosecutor or investigator, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation
supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they are related to an accusation of committing a serious or particularly serious crime or to falsification of evidence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. law, which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

292. pants. Deliberate Unlawful Arrest

For deliberate unlawful arrest, if committed by a judge, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

293. pants. Deliberate Unlawful Detention and Forced Bringing

For deliberate unlawful detention or forced bringing, if committed for mercenary purposes or out of revenge, or for other personal interest and if committed by a judge, prosecutor or employee of a pre-trial investigation institution, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

294. pants. Coercion to Give Testimony

(1)	For coercion to give testimony during interrogation, if it is associated with violence, threat of violence, mockery of the person being interrogated or committed in another manner and if committed by an official conducting pre-trial criminal proceedings, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For coercion to give testimony during interrogation, if it is associated with torture and if committed by an official conducting pre-trial criminal proceedings, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

294.	1 pants. Interference in Pre-trial Criminal Proceedings

(1)	For any influencing of an official conducting pre-trial criminal proceedings with the purpose of obstructing the initiation or conduct of pre-trial criminal proceedings or achieving the adoption of an unlawful decision, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a state official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation

supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)
(In the wording of the law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and
17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

295. pants. Interference in Adjudication of Cases

(1)	For any influencing of a judge with the purpose of obstructing lawful adjudication of a case or achieving the adoption and pronouncement of an unlawful judgment or decision —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a state official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012., 11.06.2020. and
17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

296. pants. Non-execution of Court Decision and Prosecutor's Order on Punishment

(1)	For non-execution or delay in execution of a court decision or prosecutor's order on punishment, if committed by a person who was required by law or assigned task to execute it, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a state official who had the duty to execute the court decision or prosecutor's order on punishment, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

297. pants. False Impersonation as the Accused, Victim and Witness

A person who in pre-trial criminal proceedings or in court falsely impersonated the accused, victim or witness, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

298. pants. Deliberately False Report

(1)	For a deliberately false report about the commission of a criminal offence with the purpose of causing the initiation of criminal proceedings against a specific person —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they are associated with an accusation of committing a serious or particularly serious crime or with the creation of false evidence of accusation or if committed for mercenary purposes, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

299. pants. Deliberately False Statement

For a deliberately false statement, declaration or submission to a notary or bailiff, if the submission of the statement, declaration or submission is prescribed by law, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

300. pants.

Giving Deliberately False Testimony, Opinion, Translation, Explanation and Application

(1)	For giving deliberately false testimony, opinion, translation, explanation or application in administrative offence proceedings in an institution, in pre-trial criminal proceedings, in court, to a notary or bailiff, if committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for giving deliberately false testimony, opinion, translation, explanation or application, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed during pre-trial criminal proceedings or adjudication in court of cases concerning a serious or particularly serious crime, or if they have had serious consequences, or if committed for mercenary purposes, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 27.05.2004., 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012.,
15.05.2014., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

300.	1 pants. Submission of Forged Material or Written Evidence

For submission of forged material or written evidence to an investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

301. Section. Coercion to Give False Testimony, Explanation, Opinion and Translation

(1)	For bribing or otherwise unlawfully influencing a witness, victim, person against whom criminal proceedings have been initiated, detained person, suspect, accused, applicant, expert or interpreter with the aim of causing them to give false testimony or to give a sworn false explanation to a court in an administrative case, or an opinion or to make a false translation, or to refuse to give testimony or an opinion or to make a translation —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they are connected with violence or threat of violence, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are connected with torture, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020.
which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

302. Section. Refusal to Give Testimony, Opinion and to Perform Translation

(1)	For unjustified refusal by a witness, victim or other person warned about refusal to testify to give testimony in administrative offence proceedings in an institution, pre-trial investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For unjustified refusal by an expert or interpreter to perform the duties assigned to them in administrative offence proceedings in an institution, pre-trial investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020. which
enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

303. Section. Persons Who Are Not Liable for Refusal to Give Testimony

The fiancé(e), spouse, parents, children, brothers and sisters, grandparents and grandchildren of the person subject to administrative liability, the person against whom criminal proceedings have been initiated, the detained person, the suspect, the accused, as well as the person with whom the natural person who has committed an administrative offence or criminal offence lives together and with whom they have a common (undivided) household, are not liable for refusal to give testimony.
(In the wording of the law of 11.06.2020. which enters into force on 06.07.2020.)

304. Section. Disclosure of Information Obtained in Pre-trial Criminal Proceedings

For disclosure of information obtained in pre-trial criminal proceedings without the permission of the investigator or prosecutor before the completion of such proceedings, if committed by a person who has been warned about non-disclosure of the relevant information, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

305. Section. Violation of Special Protection Regulations for Persons

(1)	For failure to observe the procedure for special protection of persons established by law, as well as for disclosure of identity data or location of a protected person, committed by a person who, in connection with the performance of official duties or other circumstances, has had knowledge of information about the specially protected person and who has been warned about non-disclosure of such information, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For intentional disclosure of the organisation, methods, tactics, means of special protection measures or information about persons involved in the implementation of protection measures, committed by a protected person, if as a result of such acts death of a person has occurred or other serious consequences have been caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(4)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if as a result thereof death of a person has occurred or other serious consequences have been caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.

(In the wording of the law of 05.05.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

306. Section. Failure to Produce Evidence

For intentional failure to produce objects, documents or other materials that may have evidentiary significance in a criminal case, if a pre-trial investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court has requested their production from a person who is not the detained person, the person against whom criminal proceedings have been initiated, the suspect, the accused, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

307. Section. Unlawful Acts with Criminal Case Materials

For theft, intentional destruction, damage or falsification of criminal case materials —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020. which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

307.	1 Section. Violation of the Prohibition on Reproduction of Criminal Case Materials and Dissemination of Their Content

For violation of the prohibition to reproduce criminal case materials in any way or the prohibition to place at the disposal of third parties or otherwise disseminate non-publishable information arising from criminal case materials, if committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for violation of the said prohibitions, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 11.10.2018. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020. which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

308. Section. Unlawful Acts with Pledged, Seized and Confiscated Property

(1)	For squandering, damaging, alienating, concealing or destroying pledged, seized or property confiscated in administrative offence proceedings, as well as for exchanging it —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a person to whom such property has been entrusted, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020. which
enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

309. pants

Illegal Transfer of Substances and Objects to and Receipt from Persons Placed in Short-term Detention and Imprisonment Facilities

(1)	For the unauthorised transfer of correspondence, money, food products or other objects or substances to persons placed in short-term detention or imprisonment facilities, or receipt from such persons, if committed by an employee of these institutions, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(2)	For the transfer of narcotic, psychotropic substances, explosives, weapons or ammunition to persons placed in short-term detention or imprisonment facilities, or receipt from such persons —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by an employee of a short-term detention or imprisonment facility, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of two to five years, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

310. pants. Escape from Short-term Detention and Imprisonment Facility

(1)	For escape from a short-term detention or imprisonment facility —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they are connected with violence or threat of violence against a guard or other official of a short-term detention or imprisonment facility, or if committed by a group of persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

311. pants. Attack in an Imprisonment Facility

For the formation of an organised group of persons who are in an imprisonment facility, or participation in such a group, in order to commit an attack or use other violence against an official of the imprisonment facility, a prisoner or any other person who is in the imprisonment facility, as well as for such an attack or violence —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011. un 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 11.punktu)

312. pants. Evasion of Enforcement of a Court Judgment

(1)	For evasion of enforcement of a court judgment imposing an obligation to perform specific acts, except for a judgment on recovery of money or return of property, after the application of a procedural sanction or penalty for non-enforcement of the judgment —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For evasion of serving a sentence of deprivation of liberty or restriction of rights —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(06.06.2019. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

313. pants. Concealment Not Promised in Advance

(1)	For concealment not promised in advance of an offender, as well as of the instruments and means of committing a crime, traces of a crime, if the crime being concealed is serious, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if the crime being concealed is particularly serious, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

314. pants. Acquisition, Storage and Disposal of Criminally Obtained Property

(1)	For the acquisition, storage or disposal of property the value of which does not exceed a substantial amount, knowing that it has been obtained criminally, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	(Izslēgta ar 22.06.2017. likumu)

(3)	(Izslēgta ar 22.06.2017. likumu)
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 22.06.2017. un
17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

315. pants. Failure to Report a Crime

For failure to report, if it is known that a serious or particularly serious crime is being prepared or committed, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 12.11.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

XXIV	nodaļa
Criminal Offences in the Service of State Institutions

316. pants. Concept of State Official

(1)	State officials shall be considered to be representatives of state authority, as well as any person who permanently or temporarily performs state or local government service duties, including in a state or local government capital company, and who has the right to make decisions binding on other persons, or who has the right to perform supervisory, control, investigative or punitive functions or to dispose of the property or financial resources of a public person or its capital company.

(2)	The President of the State, members of the Saeima, the Prime Minister, members of the Cabinet of Ministers, as well as officials of state institutions elected, appointed or approved by the Saeima and the Cabinet of Ministers, heads of local governments, their deputies and executive directors shall be considered state officials holding a responsible position.

(3)	State officials shall also be considered to be officials of international organisations, international parliamentary assemblies and international courts and persons delegated by the said institutions, as well as any person who holds a legislative, executive or judicial office in a foreign state or in any of its administrative units, regardless of whether that person is appointed or elected to the office, as well as any person who performs a public function in a foreign state, including its administrative unit, state agency or state enterprise.

(4)	A foreign state within the meaning of this Section is any territory outside the Republic of Latvia.
(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 11.12.2003., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. un 10.03.2016. likumu, kas
stājas spēkā 07.04.2016.)

This excerpt sits under: Izdevējs: Saeima Veids: likums Pieņemts: 17.06.1998. > (Nodaļa 26

317. Section. Exceeding Official Authority

(1)	For intentional acts committed by a public official that manifestly exceed the limits of rights and authority granted to the public official by law or an assigned task, if these acts have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are connected with violence or threat of violence or if they are committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are connected with torture or if they have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020.
which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

318. Section. Abuse of Official Position

(1)	For intentional acts committed by a public official by abusing their official position, if these acts have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3)	For intentional acts committed by a public official by abusing their official position, if these acts have caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(4)	For intentional acts committed by a public official by abusing their official position, if these acts have caused the death of two or more persons, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 06.06.2019., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020.
which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

319. Section. Inaction of a Public Official

(1)	For failure by a public official to perform their duties, that is, if a public official intentionally or through negligence fails to perform an act which they are required to perform by law or an assigned task, and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are committed with mercenary intent, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3)	For failure by a public official to perform their duties, if serious consequences have been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(4)	For failure by a public official to perform their duties, if the death of two or more persons has occurred as a result thereof, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to
five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 29.10.2015., 06.06.2019., 06.07.2021. and
17.12.2020. which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

320. Section. Bribery

(1)	For demanding, extorting or accepting a bribe, that is, material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, committed by a public official personally or through an intermediary for any lawful or unlawful act already performed or omission permitted, using their official position, regardless of whether the demanded, extorted, accepted or offered bribe is intended for this public official or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to two years.

(2)	For accepting a bribe or an offer thereof or for demanding or extorting a bribe, committed by a public official personally or through an intermediary before the performance or non-performance of any lawful or unlawful act, using their official position, regardless of whether the demanded, extorted, accepted or offered bribe is intended for this public official or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they are committed on a large scale or if they are committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if the bribe is accepted following its demand, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(4)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they are committed by an organised group or by a public official holding a responsible position, or if the bribe is accepted following its extortion, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to eleven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 19.11.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020. which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

This excerpt sits under: Issuer: Saeima Type: law Adopted: 17.06.1998. > (Chapter 26

321. pants. Misappropriation of a Bribe

(1)	For misappropriation of a bribe that a person has received for delivery to a public official or that they have accepted by posing as a public official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2)	For misappropriation of a bribe that a public official has received for delivery to another public official or that they have accepted by posing as another public official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation

supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if it has been committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has been committed by a public official holding a responsible position, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

322. pants. Intermediation in Bribery

(Deleted by the law of 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

323. pants. Bribe-giving

(1)	For giving, offering or promising a bribe, that is, material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, following a request therefor, personally or through an intermediary, to a public official so that they, using their official position, perform or fail to perform an action, regardless of whether the bribe given, offered or promised is intended for that public official or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have been committed on a large scale or if they have been committed by a public official, or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have been committed by an organised group,—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.
(In the wording of the law of 19.11.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 10.03.2016., 06.06.2019. and
17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

324. pants. Release of Bribe-giver and Intermediary in Bribery from Criminal Liability

(1)	A person who has given a bribe may be released from criminal liability if the bribe has been extorted from that person or if they, after giving the bribe, voluntarily report what has occurred and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence. A person who has promised or offered a bribe may be released from criminal liability if they voluntarily report what has occurred and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.

(2)	Extortion of a bribe shall be understood as a request therefor for the performance of a lawful action, as well as a request associated with threats to harm the lawful interests of a person.

(3)	An intermediary or facilitator in bribery may be released from criminal liability if they, after committing the criminal acts, voluntarily report what has occurred and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.
(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012. and 10.03.2016., which enters into force on 07.04.2016.)

325. pants. Violation of Restrictions Imposed on a Public Official

(1)	For violation of restrictions or prohibitions imposed by law on a public official, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by a public official holding a responsible position, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002.,12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022.
which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

326. Section. Unlawful Participation in Property Transactions

(1)	For facilitating or participating in a property transaction, if committed by a state official out of mercenary motives or other personal interest, to whom it is prohibited by law in connection with their official position, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed by a state official who holds a responsible position, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

326.1 Section. Trading in Influence

(1)	For offering or transferring material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature personally or through an intermediary to any person, so that they, using their official, professional or social position, unlawfully influence the actions of a state official or induce another person to unlawfully influence the actions of a state official, regardless of whether the material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, if there are no elements of the offence provided for in Section 323 of this Law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For accepting material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, or an offer thereof, or for demanding material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature for oneself or any other person, so that they, using their official, professional or social position, unlawfully influence the actions of a state official or induce another person to unlawfully influence the actions of a state official or decision-making, if there are no elements of the offence provided for in Sections 198 and
320 of this Law, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 19.11.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

326.2 Section. Unlawful Demanding and Accepting of Benefits

(1)	For unlawfully accepting material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, or an offer thereof, committed by an employee of a state or local government institution who is not a state official, or by such a person authorised by a state institution, personally or through an intermediary, for performing or not performing any action using their powers,

regardless of whether the accepted material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed on a large scale or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if related to demanding or extorting material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 19.11.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

326.3 Section. Unlawful Giving of Benefits

(1)	For offering or transferring material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature personally or through an intermediary to an employee of a state or local government institution who is not a state official, or to such a person authorised by a state institution, so that they perform any unlawful action using their powers, regardless of whether the material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed on a large scale, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 06.06.2019., 11.11.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

326.	4 Section. Release of Benefit Giver from Criminal Liability

A person who has unlawfully given a benefit may be released from criminal liability if the benefit was demanded from or extorted from that person, or if they, after committing the criminal acts, voluntarily report what has occurred and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence. A person who has offered a benefit may be released from criminal liability if they voluntarily report what has occurred and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

327. Section. Official Forgery

(1)	For forging a document or for issuing or using a forged document, knowing that the document is forged, if committed by a state official, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if committed out of mercenary motives or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.
(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

328. Section. False Official Report

For providing knowingly false information to an institution or state official who has the right to request such information, as well as for concealing a document or information or knowingly withholding it, if committed by a state official who has the duty to provide such information, and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

329. Section. Disclosure of Non-Disclosable Information

For disclosing such non-disclosable information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, if committed by a state official who has been warned about non-disclosure of the information or who is responsible for safeguarding the information in accordance with law, —
