shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 26.05.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 10.03.2016., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enter into force on 01.05.2023.)

330. pants. Disclosure of non-disclosable information after leaving office

For the disclosure of such non-disclosable information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, if a person has done so after leaving the service of a state official during the period specified in the warning about non-disclosure of information, —

shall be punished by probation supervision or by community service or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enter into force on 01.05.2023.)

XXV	nodaļa
Criminal offences in military service

331. pants. Concept of a criminal offence and its subjects in military service

Criminal offences in military service shall be deemed to be the criminal offences specified in this law directed against the established order of performance of military service, which have been committed by military personnel and persons to whom special provisions provided for in laws apply.
(In the wording of the law of 17.10.2002., which enters into force on 20.11.2002.)

332. pants. Unauthorised absence

For unauthorised absence, that is, leaving a unit or place of service of the National Armed Forces, as well as failure to appear without justifiable reason at the appointed time at the place of service, if the unauthorised absence has lasted more than one day but not longer than three days and if it has been committed during a state of war or emergency, in combat conditions, during a state of emergency declared in the event of public disorder, terrorism or armed conflict, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 14.12.2006. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

333. pants. Desertion

For desertion, that is, leaving the place of service or failure to appear at the place of service with the intention of evading active service, if it has been committed during a state of war or emergency, in combat conditions, during a state of emergency declared in the event of public disorder, terrorism or armed conflict, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 14.12.2006. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

334. pants. Evasion of active service

(1)	For evasion of the performance of active service duties by inflicting bodily injury upon oneself (self-mutilation) or by simulating illness, forging documents or otherwise deceiving, as well as for refusal to perform service duties —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same act, if it has been committed during wartime or in combat conditions, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 14.12.2006., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

335. pants. Insubordination

(1)	For insubordination, that is, for open refusal to execute a superior's order, as well as for other intentional non-execution of an order, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For insubordination, as well as for other intentional non-execution of an order, if it has been committed by a group of persons or if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For insubordination, as well as for other intentional non-execution of an order during wartime or in combat conditions —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

336. pants. Non-execution of an order

For non-execution of a superior's order through negligence, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

337. pants. Resistance to a superior and compelling him to violate service duties

(1)	For resistance to a superior, as well as to another person in connection with the performance of military service duties imposed on him or for compelling him to violate these duties, if it has been committed with violence or if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

338. pants. Violence against a subordinate

(1)	For violence against a subordinate, if as a result physical suffering has been inflicted on him, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For intentional infliction of slight bodily injury on a subordinate —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For intentional infliction of medium gravity bodily injury on a subordinate, as well as for other acts that have the character of torture, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(4)	For intentional infliction of serious bodily injury on a subordinate —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

339. pants. Insult to the honour of a military person

(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

340. pants. Beating and torture of a military person

(1)	For intentional hitting or beating, as well as for other intentional violence, which a military person has committed against a military person, if at least one of them is performing military service duties, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they are connected with the intentional infliction of slight bodily injury, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are connected with the intentional infliction of medium gravity bodily injury or if they have the character of torture, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(4)	For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they are connected with the intentional infliction of serious bodily injury,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

341. Section. Abuse of Power and Exceeding Official Authority

(1)	For abuse of power or official position or exceeding official authority committed by a superior, if this offence is committed with mercenary intent or if substantial harm is caused, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For abuse of power or official position or exceeding official authority committed by a superior, if this offence has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

342. Section. Negligence in Service

(1)	For failure to perform official duties, as well as for negligent performance thereof, if as a result substantial harm is caused,
—

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For failure to perform official duties, as well as for negligent performance thereof, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

343. Section. Squandering and Loss of Service Property

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For the sale of weapons, ammunition, means of transport, military service equipment entrusted for service use, or for the loss of these items by violating the rules for their storage, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

344. Section. Intentional Destruction and Damage of Military Service Property

(1)	For intentional destruction or damage of weapons, ammunition, means of transport, military service equipment — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation
supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

345. Section. Destruction and Damage of Military Service Property by Negligence

For destruction or damage of weapons, ammunition, means of transport or military service equipment by negligence, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

346. Section

Violation of Rules for Storage, Use, Accounting and Transportation of Weapons, Ammunition, Explosives, Radioactive and Dangerous Substances

(1)	For violation of rules for storage, use, accounting or transportation of weapons, ammunition, explosives, radioactive substances, as well as other dangerous substances, items or materials, if it has caused substantial harm, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same offence, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

347. Section. Violation of Rules for Operating and Exploiting Combat Vehicles

For violation of rules for operating or exploiting a combat vehicle, if it has caused the death of a person or other serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.
(With amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

348. Section. Violation of Rules for Operating Vessels

For violation of rules for operating a vessel, if it has caused the loss of the vessel or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

349. Section. Violation of Rules for Flights and Their Preparation

For violation of rules for a flight or its preparation, if it has caused a catastrophe or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

350. Section. Violation of Guard (Security) Regulations

(1)	For violation of guard (security) regulations or orders or instructions issued for the application of these regulations, committed while on guard duty or at posts, guarding ammunition, weapons, fuel, equipment warehouses or other facilities of significant importance, —

shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2)	For the same offence, if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

351. Section

Violation of Service Facility Regulations, Instructions or Orders

(1)	(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2)	For violation of service facility regulations, instructions or orders, if committed by a serviceman assigned to round-the-clock duty (excluding guard duty), and if it has caused serious consequences, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

352. Section. Disclosure of Military Information

For disclosure of such non-disclosable military information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation
supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 10.03.2016., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enter into force on 01.05.2023.)

353. Section. Surrender and Abandonment of Means of Warfare to the Enemy

For surrender of entrusted military forces to the enemy, as well as for abandonment of fortifications, combat equipment or other means of warfare to the enemy, when not justified by combat circumstances, commanders —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to ten years.
(With amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

354. Section. Unauthorized Abandonment of the Battlefield and Refusal to Use Weapons

For unauthorized abandonment of the battlefield during combat or refusal to use weapons in combat —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

355. pants. Criminal conduct of a prisoner of war

(1)	For violence against other prisoners of war or cruel treatment of them, if committed by a prisoner of war who holds a senior position, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.

(2)	For intentional action by a prisoner of war directed against the Republic of Latvia, the purpose of which is to support the enemy or assist the enemy, —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.
(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002 and 13.12.2012, which enter into force on 01.04.2013.)

356. Section

Unlawful Wearing and Malicious Use of the Emblems of the Red Cross, Red Crescent and Blue and White Shield

For the unlawful wearing of the emblems of the Red Cross, Red Crescent or Blue and White Shield in areas of hostilities, if committed by a person who has no right to do so, as well as for the malicious use of the emblems of the Blue and White Shield or the flag or emblems of the Red Cross or Red Crescent, or the prescribed colouring of medical evacuation means during wartime —

shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a short term or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
(As amended by the law of 18.12.2003., with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

Transitional Provisions

1.	The time and procedure for the entry into force of this law shall be determined by a special law.

2.	The Cabinet of Ministers shall develop and submit to the Saeima by 1 August 1998 a draft law "On the Time and Procedure for the Entry into Force of the Criminal Law".

3.	The Cabinet of Ministers shall develop and adopt by 1 August 1998 regulations:

1)	on access to computer programmes and information system security;

2)	on the restriction of prostitution.

4.	Sections 282.1 and 282.2 of this law shall come into force on 1 December 2003.
(As amended by the law of 17.10.2002., which comes into force on 20.11.2002.)

5.	Section 195.2 of this law shall come into force on 1 July 2006.
(As amended by the law of 08.12.2005., which comes into force on 05.01.2006.)

6.	The amendment to the fourth paragraph of Section 61 of this law regarding the mandatory obligation to participate in probation programmes shall come into force on 1 January 2013. With respect to persons convicted under Sections 159, 160, 161, 162, 162.1, 164, 165 and 166 of this law, the amendment to the fourth paragraph of Section 61 of this law regarding the mandatory obligation to participate in probation programmes shall come into force on 1 October 2011.
(As amended by the law of 30.10.2008., with amendments made by the laws of 16.06.2009. and 08.07.2011., which comes into force on 01.10.2011.)

7.	Subparagraphs 2.1 and 3 of the fourth paragraph of Section 58.1 of this law shall not be applied until 31 December 2012.
(As amended by the law of 16.06.2009., which comes into force on 01.07.2009.)

8.	The amendment to the second paragraph of Section 65 of this law regarding the reduction of the term of deprivation of liberty and the non-application of this type of punishment shall apply to minors who have committed a criminal offence after the entry into force of this amendment.
(As amended by the law of 16.06.2009., which comes into force on 01.07.2009.)

9.	The regulation provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of the transitional provisions of the law "Amendments to the Criminal Law" adopted on 21 October 2010, in the part concerning criminal proceedings pending before courts, prosecutor's offices and pre-trial investigation institutions for criminal offences qualified under Section 149, shall be applicable only with respect to those criminal proceedings in which the criminal offence is qualified under the first paragraph of Section 149 of the Criminal Law. In criminal cases pending before courts, prosecutor's offices and pre-trial investigation institutions for criminal offences committed before 31 December 2010 and qualified under the second, third and fourth paragraphs of Section 149 of the Criminal Law, the qualification of the offence shall remain unchanged and punishment shall be applied to persons in accordance with the law that was in force until 31 December 2010.
(As amended by the law of 02.12.2010., which comes into force on 01.01.2011.)

10.	The amendments to subparagraph 5 of the second paragraph of Section 36, Section 45, subparagraph 3 of the first paragraph of Section 52 and the fifth paragraph of Section 55 of this law regarding the elimination of the additional punishment — police control — shall come into force on 1 January 2015.
(As amended by the law of 08.07.2011., which comes into force on 01.10.2011.)

11.	The amendments to the third paragraph of Section 98, the fourth paragraph of Section 99, Sections 116, 117, 118, 125, the third paragraph of Section 148, the third paragraph of Section 152, the third paragraph of Section 153, the third paragraph of Section 154, the third paragraph of Section 154.1, the third paragraph of Section 165.1, Section 174, the fourth paragraph of Section 175, Section 176, the third paragraph of Section 177, the third paragraph of Section 177.1, the second paragraph of Section 183, Section 184, the second paragraph of Section 187, the third paragraph of Section 190.1, the second paragraph of Section 192, the fourth paragraph of Section 193, the third paragraph of Section 193.1, the third paragraph of Section 195, the third paragraph of Section 206, the third paragraph of Section 218, the third paragraph of Section 220.1, the third paragraph of Section 221, the third paragraph of Section 221.1, Sections 224, 225, the second paragraph of Section 231, the third paragraph of Section 233, the third paragraph of Section 243, Sections 253, 253.1, the second paragraph of Section 269, the third paragraph of Section 285, Section 311, the third paragraph of Section 314, the fourth paragraph of Section 320, the third paragraph of Section 323 of this law regarding the replacement of the additional punishment — police control — with the additional punishment — probation supervision — shall come into force on 1 January 2015.
(As amended by the law of 14.03.2013., which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

12.	The additional punishment — police control — shall continue to be applied to persons who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 159, 160, 164, 165 and 166 of this law before 30 September 2011, and to persons who have committed another criminal offence provided for in a section of the Special Part of this law before 31 December 2014.
(As amended by the law of 08.07.2011., which comes into force on 01.10.2011.)

12.	1 From 1 January 2022, for the persons referred to in paragraph 12 of these transitional provisions who have not yet been convicted, the additional punishment — probation supervision — shall be applied instead of the additional punishment — police control —, calculating two days of police control as one day of probation supervision.
(As amended by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

13.	The additional punishment — police control — shall continue to be served by persons to whom it has been imposed, and the following conditions for the execution of police control shall apply to them:

1)	if a person has been conditionally released from serving the punishment before the term, the execution of the additional punishment — police control — shall commence from the moment when the supervision of the person after conditional early release has ended;

2)	the court, upon the application of a police institution, may reduce the term of police control or cancel police control;

3)	if the convicted person has committed a new crime during the serving of the additional punishment, the court shall replace the unserved part of the additional punishment with deprivation of liberty and determine the final punishment in accordance with the provisions of Section 51 of this law, calculating two days of police control as one day of deprivation of liberty;

4)	if a person for whom police control has been determined by a court judgment maliciously violates its provisions, the court, upon the application of a police institution, may replace the unserved part of the additional punishment, calculating two days of police control as one day of deprivation of liberty. A violation of police control provisions is malicious if a person has been administratively punished twice within a year for such a violation.
(As amended by the law of 08.07.2011., with amendments made by the law of 16.10.2014., which comes into force on 01.02.2015.)

14.	The additional punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied from 1 October 2011 to persons who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 159, 160, 161, 162, 162.1, 164, 165 and 166 of this law after 30 September 2011, and from 1 January 2015 — to persons who have committed another criminal offence provided for in a section of the Special Part of this law after 31 December 2014.
(As amended by the law of 29.10.2015., which comes into force on 03.12.2015.)

14.	1 When determining punishment for several criminal offences in accordance with Section 50 of the Criminal Law or under several judgments in accordance with Section 51 of the Criminal Law and establishing that both police control and probation supervision have been imposed on a person, police control shall be included in probation supervision regardless of the term determined for police control. If probation supervision has been determined in accordance with paragraph 12.1 of these transitional provisions, then it shall be included in probation supervision that has been determined in accordance with paragraph 14 of these transitional provisions.
(As amended by the law of 10.03.2016., with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

15.	Persons for whom the death penalty has been replaced with deprivation of liberty shall be subject to those provisions of this law that apply to persons sentenced to life imprisonment.
(As amended by the law of 01.12.2011., which comes into force on 01.01.2012.)

16.	The amendments to the second paragraph of Section 78, Sections 80, 80.1, 82, 86, 87, 88, 88.2, 88.3, 89.1 and 107, the second paragraph of Section 124, the second paragraph of Section 126, the second paragraph of Section 152, the first and second paragraphs of Section 153, the first and second paragraphs of Section 154, the second paragraph of Section 154.1, the second paragraph of Section 165.1, the third paragraph of Section 179, the first paragraph of Section 183, the second paragraph of Section 185, the third paragraph of Section 190, the third paragraph of Section 191, Section 230, the second and third paragraphs of Section 251, the third paragraph of Section 252, Section 268, the second and third paragraphs of Section 309 and the second paragraph of Section 310 of this law regarding the additional punishment — probation supervision — shall come into force on 1 January 2015.
(As amended by the law of 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

17.	The regulation of the law regarding the complete or partial cancellation of imposed obligations, the execution of the punishment determined in the judgment or the extension of the probation period for a conditionally convicted person, which was in force until 31 January 2015, shall be applicable with respect to persons who were conditionally convicted before 31 January 2015.
(As amended by the law of 16.10.2014., which comes into force on 01.02.2015.)

18.	The regulation of the law regarding the execution of the unserved part of the punishment for a person conditionally released before the term, which was in force until 31 January 2015, shall be applicable with respect to persons who were conditionally released from serving the punishment before 31 January 2015.
(As amended by the law of 16.10.2014., which comes into force on 01.02.2015.)

19.	Paragraphs 2.1, 3.1, 4.1 and 4.2 of Section 61, Section 61.1 and paragraph 3.1 of Section 65 of this law shall come into force on 1 July 2015.
(As amended by the law of 15.01.2015., which comes into force on 01.02.2015.)

20.	The amendments to the title of Section 84, the disposition and sanction of the first paragraph of this law shall come into force simultaneously with the Law on International and National Sanctions of the Republic of Latvia.
(As amended by the law of 28.01.2016., which comes into force on 01.03.2016. The said amendments are included in the version of the law as of 01.03.2016.)

21.	The provisions of Chapter VIII2 of this law with respect to those criminal offences that were committed before 31 July 2017 shall be applied insofar as these provisions are not less favourable than those provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law that regulated the handling of criminally obtained property at the time of the commission of the respective criminal offence.
(As amended by the law of 22.06.2017., which comes into force on 01.08.2017.)

22.	Persons who have committed the criminal offences provided for in Sections 88, 88.1, 88.2 or 88.3 of this law before the day when the amendment regarding the deletion of these sections came into force shall be brought to criminal liability and punished in accordance with those provisions of this law that were in force at the time of the commission of the respective offence.
(As amended by the law of 26.04.2018., which comes into force on 23.05.2018.)

23.	The amendment to the disposition of the first paragraph of Section 300 of this law shall come into force simultaneously with the Administrative Liability Law.
(As amended by the law of 06.06.2019., which comes into force on 03.07.2019.)

24.	The new name "community service" for the type of punishment — forced labour — may be used when adopting decisions on the application of punishment also for such criminal offences that were committed before 31 December 2021.
(As amended by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

25.	The principal punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied from 1 January 2022 to adults who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 126, 130, 130.1, 174, 248.2, 253.2 and 340 of this law after 31 December 2021, and to minors who have committed any criminal offence provided for in the Special Part of this law after 31 December 2021.
(As amended by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

25.1 The principal punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied from 15 July 2023 to adults who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 132, 132.1 and 168.1 of this law after 14 July 2023.
(As amended by the law of 15.06.2023., which comes into force on 15.07.2023.)

25.	2 The principal punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied from 20 October 2023 to adults who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 285, 285.1 and 285.2 of this law after 19 October 2023.
(As amended by the law of 05.10.2023., which comes into force on 20.10.2023.)

26.	The amendments to the first paragraph of Section 41, the sanction of the second paragraph of Section 139, the sanction of the first and second paragraphs of Section 139.1, the sanction of paragraph 2.1 of Section 141, the sanction of paragraph 2.1 of Section 195.2, the sanction of Section 221.6 and the sanction of the first paragraph of Section 239.1 of this law regarding the principal punishment — community service and probation supervision — shall come into force on 1 January 2022.
(As amended by the law of 06.07.2021., which comes into force on 05.08.2021.)

27.	Until 31 December 2021, the type of punishment — forced labour — shall be applied instead of the type of punishment — community service — provided for in Section 218.1 of this law.
(As amended by the law of 06.07.2021., which comes into force on 05.08.2021.)

28.	Persons who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Section 322 of this law before the day when the amendment regarding the deletion of this section came into force shall be brought to criminal liability and punished in accordance with those provisions of this law that were in force at the time of the commission of the respective offence.
(As amended by the law of 07.04.2022., which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

29.	The second paragraph of Section 66.2 of this law shall not be applied from 1 July 2022 until 31 December 2024.
(As amended by the law of 16.06.2022., which comes into force on 23.06.2022.)

Informative Reference to European Union Directives
(As amended by the law of 21.10.2010., with amendments made by the laws of 16.06.2011., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 25.09.2014., 16.10.2014., 28.01.2016., 22.06.2017., 26.04.2018., 06.06.2019. and 06.07.2021., which comes into force on 05.08.2021.)

The law incorporates legal norms arising from:

1)	Council Directive 79/409/EEC of 2 April 1979 on the conservation of wild birds;

2)	Council Directive 91/308/EEC of 10 June 1991 on prevention of the use of the financial system for the purpose of money laundering;

3)	Council Directive 91/477/EEC of 18 June 1991 on control of the acquisition and possession of weapons;

4)	Council Directive 92/43/EEC of 21 May 1992 on the conservation of natural habitats and of wild fauna and flora;

5)	Council Directive 93/15/EEC of 5 April 1993 on the harmonization of the provisions relating to the placing on the market and supervision of explosives for civil uses;

6)	Directive 95/46/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 24 October 1995 on the protection of individuals with regard to the processing of personal data and on the free movement of such data;

7)	Council Directive 2000/43/EC of 29 June 2000 implementing the principle of equal treatment between persons irrespective of racial or ethnic origin;

8)	Directive 2000/59/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 27 November 2000 on port reception facilities for ship-generated waste and cargo residues;

9)	Directive 2001/97/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 4 December 2001 amending Council Directive 91/308/EEC on prevention of the use of the financial system for the purpose of money laundering;

10)	Directive 2005/35/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 7 September 2005 on ship-source pollution and on the introduction of penalties for infringements;

11)	Directive 2006/24/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 March 2006 on the retention of data generated or processed in connection with the provision of publicly available electronic communications services or of public communications networks and amending Directive 2002/58/EC;

12)	Directive 2008/99/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 19 November 2008 on the protection of the environment through criminal law;

13)	Directive 2009/123/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 21 October 2009 amending Directive 2005/35/EC on ship-source pollution and on the introduction of penalties for infringements;

14)	Directive 2009/52/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 18 June 2009 providing for minimum standards on sanctions and measures against employers of illegally staying third-country nationals;

15)	Directive 2011/36/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 5 April 2011 on preventing and combating trafficking in human beings and protecting its victims, and replacing Council Framework Decision 2002/629/JHA;

16)	Directive 2011/93/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 13 December 2011 on combating the sexual abuse and sexual exploitation of children and child pornography, and replacing Council Framework Decision 2004/68/JHA;

17)	Directive 2013/40/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 12 August 2013 on attacks against information systems and replacing Council Framework Decision 2005/222/JHA;

18)	Directive 2004/38/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 29 April 2004 on the right of citizens of the Union and their family members to move and reside freely within the territory of the Member States amending Regulation (EEC) No 1612/68 and repealing Directives 64/221/EEC, 68/360/EEC, 72/194/EEC, 73/148/EEC, 75/34/EEC, 75/35/EEC, 90/364/EEC, 90/365/EEC and 93/96/EEC;

19)	Directive 2014/57/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 16 April 2014 on criminal sanctions for market abuse (market abuse directive);

20)	Directive 2014/62/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 May 2014 on the protection of the euro and other currencies against counterfeiting by criminal law, and replacing Council Framework Decision 2000/383/JHA;

21)	Directive 2014/42/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 3 April 2014 on the freezing and confiscation of instrumentalities and proceeds of crime in the European Union;

22)	Directive (EU) 2017/541 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 March 2017 on combating terrorism and replacing Council Framework Decision 2002/475/JHA and amending Council Decision 2005/671/JHA;

23)	Directive (EU) 2017/1371 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 5 July 2017 on the fight against fraud to the Union's financial interests by means of criminal law;

24)	Directive (EU) 2018/1673 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 23 October 2018 on combating money laundering by criminal law;

25)	Directive (EU) 2019/713 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 17 April 2019 on combating fraud and counterfeiting of non-cash means of payment and replacing Council Framework Decision 2001/413/JHA.

The law was adopted by the Saeima on 17 June 1998.

President of the State G.Ulmanis

Riga, 8 July 1998
